市場焦點
美國
• 9月綜合PMI升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速 ，新訂單及就業亦
明顯成長 ，顯示消費 、AI投資與國防支出支撐景氣動能 。不過，需求快速擴張也加劇供應鏈
瓶頸，企業投入價格升至 2022年10月以來高點，燃料、運輸及原物料成本上升 ，顯示景氣韌
性與通膨壓力同步升溫，增加Fed政策維持緊縮的壓力。
美國
期中選舉
• 目前民調顯示 ，民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ，這將有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期 ，進
而帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑 ，對長天期美債形成支撐 。此外既有資料顯示 ，選後一年美股
平均報酬率達12.4%，主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到經濟成長、企業獲利及貨
幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。

越南
• 越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數 。估計此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流
入越南。資產管理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約 25億美元。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風 ，除了能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經濟成長 ，今年以來越南成為
美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成為中國商品流入的中轉國 ，美對越的301條款
調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。

美國景氣強勢擴張，通膨與利率壓力再升
• 企業活動創逾五年新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速擴張 ：S&P
Global美國9月綜合PMI初值升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高，
且連續四個月加速成長；服務業 PMI升至58.7（59個月高點）、
製造業PMI升至57.0（52個月高點），產出亦同步加速 ，加上
訂單與就業成長皆達近年最快 ，顯示內需、AI投資及國防支出
共同支撐企業活動。S&P Global依PMI調查推估經濟年成長約
5%，Q3則上看4%，反映短期景氣動能明顯增強。
• 供給受限推高成本 ，通膨壓力再升溫： 需求快速擴張同時推高
供應鏈與產能壓力 ，企業未完成訂單升至 2022年5月以來高點 ，
供應商交貨延遲亦明顯惡化 ，反映產能不足 ，投入價格指數升

至2022年10月以來新高 ，企業主要反映燃料 、運輸、原物料
及薪資成本上升 ，顯示供給受限與能源成本可能持續向終端價
格傳導，增加通膨黏著風險並提高Fed維持緊縮政策的壓力。
• 後市觀察：數據公布後，10年期美債殖利率一度升至約 5.12%，
創2007年以來新高 ，市場對10月再升息的押注亦升溫 ，短線
需關注供應鏈壓力是否緩解 ，以及後續ISM、就業與物價數據
能否確認通膨再升溫趨勢 。投資策略建議關注利率敏感度較低
的高品質成長股或防禦型資產 ，可適度布局受惠強勁內需與 AI
／國防支出的美股大型科技與工業類股 ，並留意升息環境下金
融股的利差受益機會 ，整體建議維持分散配置並嚴控利率風險 。
資料來源：S&P Global PMI, Bureau of Economic Analysis via S&P Global Market
Intelligence， 2026.9.24

能源成本升高推升通膨，利率維持高檔壓力升溫
• 能源與運輸成本上升，增加通膨黏著風險：中東衝突與
俄羅斯煉油設施遭攻擊 ，使全球柴油供應持續偏緊 ，燃
料價格及運輸成本同步上升；由於柴油廣泛應用於運輸 、
農業與製造業，價格上漲將透過物流及生產成本向下游
傳導。目前全球柴油庫存偏低 、煉油產能受限，若供應
中斷延續，可能使能源價格維持高檔並延長通膨壓力。
• 公債與企業債供給增加 ，長端利率壓力升高：美國財政
赤字及政府融資需求偏高 ，加上大型科技公司持續為 AI
資料中心等基礎建設籌資 ，債券供給增加使市場承接壓
力升高；在通膨預期未完全穩定的情況下 ，投資人對長
天期債券要求較高的期限溢酬 ，進一步推升長端殖利率 ，
同時AI相關企業債近期亦出現利差擴大 ，反映市場對大

量發債及資本支出回收期的關注升高。
• 資產配置面臨成長與通膨雙重拉扯： 能源價格、企業融
資成本及長端殖利率若持續處於高檔 ，將提高企業資金
成本並壓縮高估值資產的評價空間；同時 ，利率與能源
價格波動也可能擴大股 、債市震盪。因此資產配置建議
降低長天期債券對利率變化的敏感度 ，提高對通膨與利
率波動的防禦能力 ，並以現金流穩定、具備定價能力之
優質標的為核心。
資料來源：Federal Reserve Bank of St. Louis via FRED ， 2026.9.8
美國零售柴油價格與通膨預期走勢對比
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.9

美國期中選舉-目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數
• 目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ：最新民調顯示，民主黨
於11月美國期中選舉，取得國會多數席次的機率已領先共和黨，根據市場
情境分析，期中選舉不影響既有法案執行 ，但將左右未來兩年的財政預算 、
稅制改革 、政府支出及國會監督權限 。情境(1)若共和黨續掌國會 ，減稅
政策延長、產業回流、關稅及移民限制等議程有望加速推進 ，財政擴張力
道可能進一步增強 。情境(2)分裂國會 ，兩黨制衡將使重大法案推動難度
提高，新增減稅與大型財政刺激方案較難落地 。情境(3)民主黨全面掌控
國會，則可能提高對政府政策的監督力度 ，政策方向偏向提高稅負與監管 ，
壓抑企業獲利 ，使政策環境趨向平衡。

顧
美國期中選舉-對於股債市的影響
• 對股市的影響：美國銀行分析1900至2025年共31次美國期中選舉，資料發現標普500在期中選舉前12個月平均報酬約為2.9%，
低於所有年度平均 8.9%。因為市場在選舉前往往受到政策不確定性 、政黨競爭及投資人觀望情緒影響 ，波動相對較大 。然而
此現象不代表選舉必然導致市場疲弱，因為當時的經濟環境、通膨、利率及企業獲利等因素同樣扮演重要角色。期中選舉後12
個月的股市表現通常較佳 。歷史數據顯示，選後一年美股平均報酬率達12.4%。主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到
經濟成長、企業獲利及貨幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。
• 對債市的影響：若民主黨取得國會主導權，市場普遍預期財政支出擴張幅度將較受節制 ，有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期，進而

帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑，對長天期美債形成支撐，若共和黨持續全面掌控國會，市場可能預期減稅及財政擴張政策延續，
進一步推升財政赤字預估，帶動公債殖利率走高，對長天期美債形成壓力，不過市場已部分反映相關預期，實際衝擊幅度有限。
美國期中選舉前後對於美股的影響
資料來源：美國銀行，2026.7
資料來源：復華投信，2026.9

AI建設熱潮吞噬自由現金流
• 資本支出創新高：雲端服務巨頭(包括亞馬遜、Alphabet、微軟、Meta、甲骨文以及SpaceX等)正在全力擴充AI算力與資料
中心，2026年大型雲端服務巨頭的資本支出合計預估將接近 9,600億美元，而2025至2031年的累計AI資本支出更可能逼近
9兆美元。
• 自由現金流出現赤字： 高額的投資加上股東回報(股息與庫藏股買回)，使許多巨頭的自由現金流(FCF)轉為負值(如Alphabet、
亞馬遜、微軟、Meta及甲骨文等)，預計到2027年，部分企業將更加依賴外部融資，而非單靠內部現金生成。
• 債券發行量大增： 為了填補資金缺口 ，科技業債券發行規模急遽上升 ，2026年迄今，各幣別的投資級科技債券發行量已突
破4,000億美元，創下歷史新高。

科技產業AI資本支出規模最大企業
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.23

獲利韌性與金庫深度：強勁EBITDA成為信用安全網
• 上兆美元的流動性護城河： 儘管債務增加，但科技十二巨頭合計持有近 1兆美元的等值現金，整體科技板塊流動性充裕 ，短
期內無流動性危機。
• EBITDA強勁成長消化債務：市場預期覆蓋的12家最大科技企業在2026與2027年的EBITDA合計將成長約35%，強勁的獲利
能力顯著降低槓桿比率的上升壓力，確保信評機構維護穩定的信用評級。
• 資本配置策略分化：(1)兼顧股東回報：蘋果、微軟、Meta與Alphabet在投入AI同時，仍維持大規模的庫藏股買回與股息發
放。(2)全數投入資本支出：亞馬遜將絕大部分現金導向資本支出(2026年資本支出預計達2,200億美元居首)，且不發放股息。
• 小結與投資建議：科技巨頭為維持AI技術與基礎設施領先優勢，短期內，負自由現金流將成為常態。然而，這並非財務危機

訊號，而是企業利用超強獲利能力 (高EBITDA)與龐大的現金儲備進行戰略性擴張 ，科技板塊的信用風險目前處於可控且健
康的狀態，投資建議聚焦AI變現能力與EBITDA成長，優先佈局具有強大獲利護城河且能將算力轉化為實質營收的龍頭企業 。

越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數
• 預估引導60億美元資金流入越南： 富時羅素估計，此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流入越南 。受此提振，資產管
理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約25億美元。整個指數過渡將分為四個階段推行至2027年：
今年9月率先納入10%，明年3月追加20%，最後於明年6月與9月分別完成剩餘各35%的分配。市場分析指出，隨著明年3月
配額加大，市場關注度與對本地投資人的影響將更為顯著。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風： 儘管官方早前誓言今年 GDP成長目標達10%，但中東衝突以來能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經
濟成長。此外，越南與美國的貿易協商仍在進行中，由於今年以來越南成為美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成

為中國商品流入的中轉國，美對越的301條款調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。
• 觀點：儘管短線面臨不確定性 ，但越南股市同時具備多項利多題材 ，包含企業獲利成長、公共投資加速、FDI回流及資本市
場改革等。中長期結構仍偏正向，可以衛星配置的角度布局，或是以定期定額的方式參與長期成長潛力。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.09。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。

資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。

非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良

息日前一日之淨值×100%。

## Document 068: 2026年9月经济数据点评：外强内弱延续，政策脉冲待传导

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年9月经济数据点评：外强内弱延续，政策脉冲待传导.pdf
Report source: 联合资信评估
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

www.lhratings.com  研究报告 1

外强内弱延续，政策脉冲待传导 —
2026 年 9 月经济数据点评 
联合资信 主权部  
前三季度 GDP 同比增长 4.6%， 三季度回升至4.5%， 经济在二季度减速后温和企稳。9 月
延续 “外需与高技术制造引领、 内需与传统部门偏弱” 的分化格局： 出口再创新高、AI 链主导
且动能向多品类扩散； 消费边际改善但汽车拖累显著， 投资与房地产仍在寻底； 工业增加值加
快，但增长高度集中于高技术制造；PPI 创年内新高，CPI 与 PPI 剪刀差重新扩大；新增社融
超预期但主要由政府债支撑， 信贷仍偏弱。 政策端增量工具加快落地， 但向实体需求的传导仍
待观察。整体处于政策驱动向内生修复过渡的“缺口期” ，修复持续性取决于消费与房地产的
企稳节奏。

### Page 2

www.lhratings.com  研究报告 2 
2026 年前三季度 GDP 同比增长 4.6%，分季度看，一季度增长 5.0%，二季度放
缓至 4.3%， 三季度回升至4.5%， 环比增长1.2%， 经济在经历二季度减速后于三季度
温和企稳。9 月数据延续“外需与高技术制造引领、内需与传统部门偏弱”的分化格
局： 出口再创新高、 工业增加值加快增长， 但消费仅边际改善， 固定资产投资与房地
产仍在寻底，PPI 与 CPI 剪刀差重新扩大，社融总量承压而结构优化。整体而言，经
济处于由政策驱动向内生修复过渡的“缺口期”，修复的持续性取决于消费与房地产
的企稳节奏，而非外需或政策脉冲的单一强度。 
一、出口：再创新高、AI 链主导、动能向多品类扩散 
9 月，按美元计价，中国出口总值同比增长 36.3%，前值为 25.0%；进口总值同
比增长 31.0%，前值为 28.2%；贸易顺差 1335.3 亿美元，较去年同期增加 448.1 亿美
元，创 2025 年 1 月以来最高水平。 
出口的强劲表现仍高度集中于 AI 相关产业链， 但增长动能开始向更多品类扩散。
9 月集成电路出口同比增长 136.8%， 增速较上月加快6.2 个百分点； 自动数据处理设
备及其零部件同比增长 93.3%，增速加快 16.3 个百分点，两者合计拉动出口增长约
13 个百分点。与此同时， 增长动能向更多品类扩散：传统消费品出口明显回暖， 玩具
增长 40.8%， 服装、 鞋靴、 家具分别增长24.1%、20.3%和 22.1%， 服装、 鞋靴、 箱包、
玩具和家具合计拉动出口增长 1.9 个百分点， 低基数效应及对美出口修复可能是重要
原因。 
分区域看，对东盟出口增速升至 52.3%， 对韩国出口增速达68.2%， 均创2021 年
2 月以来新高； 对美出口在低基数效应及中美经贸关系边际缓和支撑下回升至 39.6%，
增速创 2021 年 3 月以来新高；对欧盟出口增速大幅回升至 25.3%，为今年 2 月以来
最高水平， 但9 月中国对欧盟贸易顺差扩大至 348 亿美元， 创有记录以来新高， 需关
注欧盟在 10 月中旬中欧贸易磋商关键窗口期的政策表态是否升级贸易限制措施。

数据来源：海关总署、Wind，联合资信整理

二、内需：消费边际改善但结构分化延续，投资与房地产仍在寻底 
9 月，社会消费品零售总额同比增长 1.1%，较 8 月的 0.4%回升 0.7 个百分点。
剔除汽车以外的消费品零售额同比增长 3.9%，较 8 月的 2.5%明显改善，反映非汽车
类消费的修复动能在增强。 但汽车类仍是最大拖累：9 月汽车类零售额同比下降 21.2%，
降幅较 8 月的 18.5%进一步扩大。消费数据的边际改善部分源于低基数和以旧换新政
策的脉冲效应， 汽车等大宗消费的收缩尚未出现拐点， 商品消费的内生修复基础仍不
牢固。 
固定资产投资方面，1—9 月全国固定资产投资（不含农户）同比下降 7.2%，与
1—8 月降幅持平， 跌势未进一步加深， 但仍处于深度收缩区间。 分产业看， 第三产业
投资下降 10.0%， 仍是最大拖累； 第二产业投资下降2.7%， 其中制造业投资下降2.1%，
基础设施投资下降 4.2%。从资金端看，截至 9 月末，新增专项债累计发行完成全年
4.4 万亿元额度的约 76.9%，较上年同期进度稍缓但仍维持较大规模。专项债发行与
投资增速回稳 之间的时滞，表明资金从财政体系到项目落地的传导效率仍是投资端
修复的关键瓶颈。 
房地产市场方面，1—9 月全国房地产开发投资同比下降 20.6%，降幅较 1—8 月
扩大 0.7 个百分点； 其中住宅投资下降20.5%。1—9 月新建商品房销售面积同比下降
13.2%，销售额同比下降 13.9%，投资端与销售端同步走弱，且销售额降幅大于面积
降幅， 意味着房价仍在下行， 以价换量未能有效扭转市场预期。 二手房交易网签面积
同比增长 10.7%，与新房销售的下滑形成分化， 部分需求正从新房市场向二手房转移，
但这并未改变房地产开发投资深度收缩的整体格局。房地产市场的筑底过程仍在延续，
投资端对固定资产投资的拖累短期内难以消退。

数据来源：国家统计局、Wind，联合资信整理

三、工业：增长加快但结构高度集中 
9 月规模以上工业增加值同比增长 5.5%，较 8 月的 5.2%加快 0.3 个百分点，环
比增长 0.61%。生产端的扩张在总量层面是明确的， 但拆开门类与行业结构， 增长的
来源高度集中，传统部门的下行压力并未消退。 
三大门类中， 制造业增加值增长6.0%， 显著高于整体增速； 采矿业仅增长1.3%，
电力、热力、燃气及水生产和供应业增长 5.6%。制造业内部的分化更为鲜明：计算
机、通信和其他电子设备制造业增长 16.4%，专用设备制造业增长 12.1%，均显著高
于整体工业增速。与之对照，有色金属冶炼和压延加工业下降 5.1%，煤炭开采和洗
选业下降 3.3%，非金属矿物制品业下降 2.4%，黑色金属冶炼仅微增 1.6%。41 个大
类行业中 31 个保持增长，增长的覆盖面并不窄，但增速的“高度”几乎完全由高技
术制造和设备制造撑起，原材料部门则在负增长区间继续收缩。 
产品产量数据进一步印证了这一结构特征。9 月汽车产量同比下降 5.7%， 但其中
新能源汽车增长 16.9%；集成电路产量增长 18.4%；工业机器人增长 18.9%。而 9 月
原油加工量下降 8.4%， 水泥下降5.3%， 钢材下降3.8%。与能源转型、 设备更新和出
口链相关的产品保持双位数增长， 与传统基建和房地产周期绑定的重化工业品则延续
收缩。 
一个值得警惕的信号来自产销衔接。9 月出口交货值同比增长 17.0%，外需对工
业产出的吸收仍在发挥重要作用。生产端的景气在很大程度上依赖高技术制造业的高
增长和出口链的拉动，而内需对工业品的消化能力并未同步跟上 。

数据来源：国家统计局、Wind，联合资信整理

四、价格：PPI 创年内新高，剪刀差重新扩大
9 月 CPI 同比上涨 0.9%， 涨幅较8 月的 0.8%扩大 0.1 个百分点； 环比上涨0.2%，
涨幅较上月回落 0.2 个百分点。扣除食品和能源价格的核心 CPI 同比上涨 1.0%，涨
幅与上月相同。CPI 同比涨幅的扩大主要由能源价格驱动。受国际油价高位运行影响，
国内汽油价格同比上涨 16.6%，涨幅较上月大幅扩大 7.3 个百分点，影响 CPI 同比上
涨约 0.47 个百分点。服务价格同比上涨 0.8%，涨幅与上月持平；但环比由上月上涨
0.1%转为下降 0.2%，主要受暑期结束影响，飞机票、宾馆住宿和旅行社收费价格分
别下降 8.7%、7.0%和 6.0%。 食品价格同比仍为负增长， 同比下降1.7%， 降幅较上月
扩大 0.3 个百分点，其中猪肉和鲜菜价格分别下降 10.0%和 8.1%。AI 相关高技术产
品价格对 CPI 的正向贡献进一步显性化： 数据存储设备、 平板电脑、 计算机和移动电

话机价格同比分别上涨 46.7%、23.6%、21.8%和 14.9%， 涨幅均有扩大， 合计影响CPI
同比上涨约 0.23 个百分点。
PPI 方面，9 月全国工业生产者出厂价格同比上涨 5.0%，涨幅较 8 月的 3.8%大
幅扩大 1.2 个百分点， 创年内新高； 环比上涨1.2%， 涨幅较上月扩大0.8 个百分点，
由 8 月的微涨转为明显上行。PPI 同比涨幅的显著扩大由输入性因素与国内产业因素
共振驱动。 输入性方面， 国际原油价格高位运行带动石油和天然气开采业价格同比上
涨 24.5%，石油煤炭及其他燃料加工业上涨 21.6%，化学原料和化学制品制造业上涨
12.1%。国内因素方面，煤炭开采和洗选业价格同比上涨 35.3%，有色金属矿采选业
和冶炼压延加工业均上涨 21.4%。 新动能相关行业价格延续上行， 计算机通信和其他
电子设备制造业同比上涨 5.8%，电气机械及器材制造业上涨 6.2%。上述 8 个主要行
业合计影响 PPI 同比上涨约 5.42 个百分点。
CPI 与 PPI 的同比剪刀差在 9 月重新扩大。8 月剪刀差为 3.0 个百分点，9 月扩

大至 4.1 个百分点。剪刀差扩大的方向与 8 月“有所收窄”的趋势相反，核心原因在
于 PPI 的上行动力远强于 CPI。 价格信号的传导仍然不畅， 上游涨价高度集中于能源、
煤炭和有色金属等资源品领域， 中下游传统制造业和消费品行业的价格传导依然有限。
能源和资源品价格的脉冲式上行拉大了工业品与消费品之间的价格裂口， 核心CPI 的
平稳表现则说明终端需求的真实修复仍不足以形成全面的通胀传导链条。

五、金融：社融超预期回升，政府债支撑，私人需求待修复 
9 月金融数据呈现“新增社融超预期回升、信贷总量仍偏弱但结构边际改善”的
格局。9 月新增社会融资规模 3.55 万亿元， 环比大幅增加1.89 万亿元， 同比多增192
亿元， 表现好于市场预期； 社融存量同比增速为7.1%，较8 月末的 7.2%小幅回落 0.1
个百分点。 前三季度社融增量累计为27.46 万亿元， 比上年同期少2.63 万亿元， 其中
对实体经济发放的人民币贷款同比少增 3.04 万亿元，社融口径下信贷收缩仍是主要
拖累。 
信贷需求仍偏弱， 但季末冲量带动环比大幅改善。9 月人民币贷款增加 1.08 万亿
元，环比大幅增加 1.02 万亿元，同比少增 2100 亿元。从结构看，企业端改善较为明
显： 企 （事） 业单位贷款增加1.29 万亿元， 同比多增700 亿元。居民端则持续疲弱：
9 月新增住户贷款为零，同比大幅减少 3890 亿元。消费贴息政策扩围落地和重点城
市楼市活跃度提高对居民信贷形成一定拉动， 但商品消费恢复有限、 房地产市场延续
分化格局，居民加杠杆意愿仍受到明显约束。政府债券仍是社融的核心支撑项。9 月
政府债券净融资 1.53 万亿元，同比多增 3445 亿元，在当月社融中的占比达 43%。与
此同时，政府融资增速结束了连续 13 个月的下行，在 9 月转为上行，财政部已安排
使用地方政府债务结存限额 5500 亿元，四季度政府融资规模大概率高于去年同期，
政府融资的拐点基本得以确认。但企业债券表现不及预期。企业债券净融资当月减少
425 亿元，为年内罕见负增。自 2022 年以来，企业债券融资在 9 月表现较弱，但只
有 2024 年负增长，2026 年再次负增长，这一情况后续值得密切跟踪观察。 
M2 与 M1 增速双双回落，资金活化程度走弱。9 月末 M2 同比增长 7.2%，较 8
月回落 0.3 个百分点；M1 同比增长 3.2%，较8 月回落 0.9 个百分点。M2 增速下滑主
要源于贷款增速回落，信贷派生的货币减少；M2 当月新增 2.82 万亿元，同比少增

政策层面，9 月的核心变化是增量政策从“部署”转向“落地”。9 月 28 日国务
院常务会议明确推出增量政策， 次日央行即落地四项工具。 往后看， 四季度财政端的
地方债务结存限额动用构成增量政策的下一个着力点。但在私人部门信贷需求仍弱、
专项债向实物工作量转化尚在途中的背景下，政策加力的效果更可能体现为“托底”
而非“反转” ，其向实体需求的传导效率仍是判断全年经济走势的关键变量。

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 看好記憶體需求緊俏有利報價走升，維持「買進」 投資評等，目標價 800 元(6 x 27(F)PER)，
潛在上漲空間 51.8%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/10/12 收盤價： 527.00 ➢ 26Q3 營收 1336.5 億元新台幣， QoQ+61.9%，YoY+ 
611.7%，毛利率 85.7% ，營業費用 75.88 億元，營業利
益為 1068.9億元 ， 業外收入24.38億 ， 稅後淨利為 876.3
億元﹐EPS 為 25.4 元，每股淨值為 118.99元。營收成
長及毛利雙雙成長受惠 平均銷售單價季增超過 六十位數
百分比，而季減中個位數百分比，匯率有利低個位數百分
比。 
➢ 展望 26Q4，受惠 AI 需求續強預期 DRAM 將持續缺貨，
且南亞科 AI相關產品已佔營收逾 20%，雖受限產能滿載
出貨量將無法再提升，然而預期合約價將持續於 26Q4上
漲 15~20%，營收及毛利率將持續創高。因此兆豐預估營
收 1556.04 億元，QoQ+16.4%，YoY+417.1%，毛利率 
88.1%，EPS為 30.35 元。 
➢ 展望 2026~2027 年，受惠 AI 需求持續強勁三大原廠於
DRAM產能持續滿載，預期 DDR5、DDR4、DDR3等產
品將持續供不應求。1C 產品預期於 27Q1 開始驗證，有
利打入 AI伺服器供應鏈，預期 AI 占比將逐季提高。因此
兆豐預估營收 4208.9/6993.1 億元，YoY+532.1/66.2%，
毛利率 83.3/86.3%，EPS 為 77.85/133.2 元。南亞科目

營 運 分 析

南亞科技為全球前四大 DRAM廠商 
南亞科技主要致力 DRAM (動態隨機存取記憶體)的研發、設計、製造與銷售，
開發自主技術，發展智慧財產權，培育關鍵技術研發團隊，落實綠色科技及
智慧製造全面提升製程效能與產能，是全球第4大的 DRAM公司。公司已成
功開發 10 奈米級 DRAM 製程技術，將可微縮至少 3 個世代，並用以發展
DDR5及 LPDDR5 等下世代產品，強化公司自主技術開發與製程轉換佈局。
第二世代 10 奈米級製程技術(1B)產品 16Gb DDR5 已完成設計與試產，於
2024 年量產。南亞科技具備完整 DRAM 產品線，可因應智慧世代的發展，
滿足多元化的產品需求。預期 1C產品於 27Q1 開始驗證。

26Q3 ASP成長帶動營收毛利雙雙上升 
26Q3 營收  1336.5 億元新台幣， QoQ+61.9%，YoY+ 611.7%，毛利率
85.7% ，營業費用 75.88 億元，營業利益為 1068.9 億元，業外收入 24.38
億，稅後淨利為 876.3 億元﹐EPS 為 25.4 元，每股淨值為 118.99元。營
收成長及毛利雙雙成長受惠平均銷售單價季增超過六十位數百分比，而季減
中個位數百分比，匯率有利低個位數百分比。

26Q4合約價持續上漲  
展望 26Q4，受惠 AI 需求續強預期 DRAM 將持續缺貨，且南亞科 AI 相關產
品已佔營收逾 20%，雖受限產能滿載出貨量將無法再提升，然而預期合約價
將持續於 26Q4 上漲 15~20%，營收及毛利率將持續創高。因此兆豐預估營
收 1556.04 億元，QoQ+16.4%，YoY+417.1%，毛利率 88.1%，EPS 為 30.35
元。 
圖二、26Q4 預期記憶體報價持續走升 
南亞科各季營收及獲利走勢預估(單位：百萬)

資料來源：南亞科；兆豐投顧預估

季增超過六十位數百

- 4 - 
個股報告

圖三、DRAM合約價報價走勢圖

資料來源：兆豐投顧整理

南亞科完成 787.2 億元私募案，攜手四大客戶搶佔 AI 供應鏈先機 
南亞科日前完成總額新台幣  787.2 億元的私募認購，吸引四大核心客戶參
與。增資後，相關投資方持股比例達 10.19%。雙方同時簽署供應協議，確
保未來能穩定供應多元 DRAM 產品組合。主要目的在於強化南亞科與客戶
的戰略合作，透過 e-SSD 與網通產品等領域，建立 AI 及雲端服務供應商
（CSP）供應鏈中的雙贏局面。私募資金將注資新廠以提升產能，進一步確
立南亞科在 AI 價值鏈中的市場地位。本次私募後，南亞科總發行股數將達 
34.5 億股。

記憶體供不應求，產能持續擴充 
目前產能為 6.5 萬片/月，產能利用率維持滿載，2026Q1~Q3 資本支出 276
億，2026 以不超過 697 億元為上限，設備佔比五成．5A新廠第一階段 35,900
片/月 於 2026~2029 資本支出約 3,466 億．新廠預計於 2027 年初開始裝機，
主要生產 1B/1C/1D製程，預期 27年底開出 20k/月，2028 年達每月投片量
達 30k/月，主要因應網通、eSSD等產品之強勁需求。

AI 帶動記憶體強勁需求，有利報價持續走升 
展望 2026~2027 年，受惠 AI 需求持續強勁三大原廠於 DRAM 產能持續滿
載，預期DDR5、DDR4、DDR3等產品將持續供不應求。1C產品預期於 27Q1
開始驗證，有利打入 AI 伺服器供應鏈，預期 AI 占比將逐季提高。因此兆豐
預估營收 4208.9/6993.1 億元，YoY+532.1/66.2%，毛利率83.3/86.3%，EPS
為 77.85/133.2 元。

記憶體持續供不應
求，報價持續走升。 
2027 新產能主要 用
於生產 1B/1C/1D 製
程。 
大型私募案確保未來
穩定供應多元 DRAM
產品組合。

Idea
Greater China Technology Hardware  | Asia Pacific
Higher OE Estimates, Upgrade
Largan and Genius
Following our semi team's increase in optical engine (OE)
estimates, we raise our fiber array unit (FAU) estimates
accordingly and thus upgrade Largan to OW and Genius to EW.
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                               +852 2239-7689
Betty Chen
Research Associate

Betty.H.Chen@morganstanley.com                                               +852 2239-7213
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
What’s Changed
Genius Electronic Optical Co.
Ltd. (3406.TW) From To
Rating Underweight Equal-weight
Price Target NT$910.00 NT$1,070.00
Largan Precision (3008.TW) From To
Rating Equal-weight Overweight
Price Target NT$7,100.00 NT$9,040.00
Raising FAU estimates after upstream semi team's OE estimates changes: Our OE

shipment forecasts increase by 128%/52% to 17.73mn/73.87mn in 2027/28 (see full
report). As OE and FAU are 1:1, we raise our FAU volume forecasts accordingly, while
key other assumptions are unchanged, which resulted in an FAU TAM increase  from
US$778mn to US$1.773bn in 2027e and from US$4.86bn to US$7.387bn in 2028e.
This significant FAU TAM increase leads to the changes below.
We now value Largan at NT$9,040/share, or 36% upside potential: Our analysis

suggests that Largan's share price already embeds >30% global market share in FAU
before formal production starts in 2H27 or even 2028. But our revised assumptions
suggest further upside if Largan successfully ramps up production and gains
meaningful market share. To reflect 3Q earnings miss, we trim our intrinsic value
estimates from NT$3,000 to NT$2,800 per share for the company's traditional
business, while our TAM analysis derives a value of NT$6,240/share  for CPO/FAU

new business, bringing the price target to NT$9,040. Considering the stronger
growth potential in 2028, we upgrade the stock to OW from EW.
We now value Genius at NT$1,070/share, upgrade to EW: Similarly, we believe
Genius's share price is also affected by hopes for FAU components.  Using a similar
approach, we conclude that the aggregate value is NT$1,070/share (NT$600 for
existing business and NT$470/share for the FAU component business). We therefore

see a more balanced risk/reward and hence move to EW.
Key risks to our call: While upstream semi capacity and yield might have already
improved, we believe the yield of FAU coupling/matching will be a key overhang.
Any lower-than-expected yield could result in  shipment misses for FAU and OE. If
the yield is not ideal, key customers are less likely to place large orders despite
upstream capacity being ready. On the upside, earlier-than-expected mass

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We trim Largan's traditional business value to NT$2,800/share but raise FAU
business to NT$6,240/share, from which we derive a new PT of NT$9,040.
We value Genius's traditional business at NT$600/share and FAU business at NT
$470/share, from which we derive a new PT of NT$1,070.
We are more positive on FAU's TAM but, we think FAU's coupling/matching could

be an overhang to our higher OE/FAU shipment estimates in 2027-28.
October 12, 2026 09:00 PM GMT

Executive Summary
Higher optical engine shipment assumptions as yields
improve
Our semi team has lifted their optical engine shipment assumptions following their latest
supply-chain checks. While PIC production remains a bottleneck, improving SoIC yields
and downstream assembly yields should support a faster OE ramp. They now expect OE
production to reach 2mn/59mn/148mn units in 2026/27/28, with actual OE shipments
reaching 0.39mn/17.73mn/73.87mn units, respectively. In particular, they assume the SoIC

yield improves from 50% in 2026 to 95% in 2027 onwards, while downstream assembly
yield rises from 20% in 2026 to 30%/50% in 2027/28. We believe this provides greater
confidence in the CPO volume ramp, although downstream assembly and testing remain
key gating factors for how quickly OE shipments can scale.

Largan: Larger CPO/FAU upside; upgrade to OW
Based on the semi team's assumption revision, we lift our CPO optical engine volume
assumptions for 2027-28e while keeping other assumptions unchanged:
1. We forecast CPO optical engine volume to increase from 0.39mn in 2026 to
17.73mn in 2027, with a further surge to 73.87mn in 2028. Consequently, FAU
(Fiber Array Units – vital optical interconnect components that align multiple

optical fibers with silicon chips, making them critical for Co-Packaged Optics)
volumes for CPO also grow from 0.39mn to 73.87mn in the same period;
2. We assume FAU ASP of US$100 in 2026-28 (average market level);
3. We forecast Largan's FAU market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in
2027 and 40% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 30% in 2028, we forecast FAU earnings
of US$9mn in 2027 and US$882mn in 2028.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
If we value the FAU business at 35x 2028e P/E (industry peers are at 40-50x P/E, but we
use a more conservative multiple to reflect the first year of scalable production), this
implies an incremental market cap of US$30.862bn. Discounting this back to 2026 at a
10% rate, we derive a per share positive impact of NT$6,240.

3Q26 results miss; revising down traditional business estimates. Largan reported 3Q26

revenue of NT$15.65bn, down 11% YoY, with gross margin declining to 42.2% and net
income of NT$5.18bn, down 27% YoY. The weaker-than-expected gross margin, mainly due
to lower new-product yields and higher structural green-power costs, resulted in a
meaningful earnings miss. Together with softer smartphone order momentum, we lower
our 2026-28 revenue estimates by 5-6% and gross margin assumptions to
45.8%/44.5%/47.5%, resulting in 12%/16%/9% downward revisions to our 2026-28

earnings estimates, respectively. For more details, please refer to Earnings Estimate
Revision                  section.
We remain positive on Largan's CPO/FAU opportunity given the company's strong track
record in high-precision manufacturing relative to most Taiwan peers. Following our semi
team's higher optical engine shipment assumptions, we see a meaningfully larger FAU
addressable market in 2027-28. More recently, Largan indicated that its first FA project

has effectively passed customer verification and that it is expanding from FA into FAU,
including coupling and potential FAU-to-carrier assembly, with dedicated capacity
expected to support mass production from around mid-2027/2H27. While the current
share price already reflects some CPO/FAU expectations, our revised assumptions suggest
further upside if Largan successfully ramps production and gains meaningful market share.
We set our price target at NT$9,040, comprising NT$2,800 from the traditional business

and NT$6,240 from the CPO/FAU business.
Genius: Higher CPO/FAU optionality; upgrade to EW

### Page 4

M
Idea
4
Similar to Largan, following semi-team's higher assumptions, we also lift our CPO optical
engine volume assumptions for 2027-2028e while keeping other assumptions unchanged.
1. We apply the same FAU volume and ASP assumptions as we do for Largan.
2. We expect Genius to be involved in FAU components, rather than overall
solutions; therefore, we assume components TAM to be 15% of the FAU TAM.
3. We forecast Genius's market share to increase from 0% in 2026 to 5% in 2027 and

30% in 2028.
4. Assuming net margins of 10% in 2027 and 25% in 2028, we forecast FAU
component earnings of US$1mn in 2027 and US$81mn in 2028.
If we value the FAU component business at 25x 2028e P/E (lower multiple than Largan
because Genius has smaller scale), it could introduce incremental market cap of US
$2,004mn. Discounting it back to 2026 at a 10% discounted rate, we derive a per-share
positive impact of NT$470.

Similar to Largan, our residual income model derives an intrinsic value of NT$600/share
for the traditional business, while the TAM analysis suggests CPO/FAU-related
opportunities could be valued at NT$470/share. Adding these together, we raise our price
target materially to NT$1,070.
Given Largan's traditional business has faced margin headwinds in 3Q26 due to the
application of green power, it's reasonable to expect that Genius could also face similar

customer requirements on green power. However, as Genius has more production capacity
in mainland China, we believe it could face less significant cost pressure. We therefore
maintain our estimates unchanged at this stage and will monitor margin development in
the upcoming quarterly results.

Earnings Estimate Revision
Largan reported 3Q26 revenue of NT$15.65bn (-11% YoY) and net income of NT$5.18bn (-
27% YoY), with gross margin declining to 42.2%. Following weaker-than-expected 3Q26
results, softer smartphone order momentum and a lower gross margin outlook due to
new-product yield pressure and higher green-power costs, we lower our revenue and GPM
estimates for 2026-28, resulting in 9-16% downward earnings revisions over the period.

Valuation and Price Target Revisions
Largan: For the traditional business, we lower our earnings and medium-term growth rate;
as a result, our intrinsic value in our base case scenario is adjusted down to NT$2,800
from NT$3,000. We use RIM valuation methodology for Greater China Hardware Tech
companies in our coverage. We apply an unchanged CoE of 8.5%, a medium-term growth
rate of 8% (from 7% previously on higher 2026e estimates), and a long-term growth rate

of 3% (unchanged). However, we believe the upward revision to optical engine shipment
assumptions materially increases the addressable market and earnings potential for
Largan's FAU business. Therefore, we add the base case per share positive value from FAU
based on the analysis in the previous section. The new SOTP valuation derives a new price
target at NT$9,040 (from NT$7,100). We also raise our bull case and bear case values to
NT$11,426 (from NT$11,142.80)  and NT$4,620 (from NT$3,649.20).

Genius: For the traditional business, we make no earnings and intrinsic value revisions; as
a result, our intrinsic value under our base case scenario is unchanged at NT$600. We use
RIM valuation methodology for Greater China Hardware Tech companies in our coverage.
We apply a CoE of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 4%, and a long-term growth rate
of 3%. However, we believe the upward revision to optical engine shipment assumptions
increases the potential value of Genius's FAU opportunity, although execution visibility

remains lower than for more established suppliers. Therefore, we add the base case per
share positive value from FAU based on the analysis in the previous section. The new
SOTP valuation derives a new price target at NT$1,070 (from NT$910). We also raise our
bull case and bear case values to NT$1,834 (from NT$1,705.20) and NT$658 (from NT
$592.40).

Risk Reward - Genius Electronic Optical Co. Ltd. (3406.TW)
NT$1,070.00
Base case: Our price target is based on an SOTP valuation, comprising the traditional
business and potential FAU opportunity. We value the traditional business using a residual
income model, consistent with our Greater China Tech coverage, assuming an 8.5% cost of
equity, a medium-term net proﬁt CAGR of 4% and a 3% terminal growth rate. We separately
value the FAU component opportunity based on our TAM and market-share assumptions.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Takeaways on NPO from 2026
OCP Global Summit
Morgan Stanley Asia Limited+
Andy Meng, CFA
Equity Analyst
Andy.Meng@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7689
Betty Chen
Research Associate
Betty.H.Chen@morganstanley.com                                                +852 2239-7213
Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
On Day 2 of the 2026 OCP Global Summit, an IPEC optical interconnect workshop

focused on discussions around NPO. Industry participants such as Source Photonics,
Lightcounting, Alibaba, Meta, Oracle, Microsoft, Ciena, Ligent, Genuine Optics,
Lumentum, Lumilens, NVIDIA and Huawei presented their views on NPO.
The industry has formed the view connectivity, rather than compute alone, is
becoming the primary bottleneck. Near-packaged optics (NPO) move optical
conversion closer to compute, targeting better signal integrity, bandwidth, and

energy efficiency. OpenCPX explicitly targets common mechanical, electrical,
optical, thermal, and management interfaces across suppliers, providing the
ecosystem with a shared implementation framework rather than only a broad
architectural vision.
However, we also observed debate over whether pluggable NPO offers materially
better flexibility and serviceability than CPO. Key concern on CPO is that optical
failure could result in scrapping an entire ASIC assembly.

Overall, we observed growing discussion and interests in NPO, implying a greater
likelihood of accelerated commercialization over the next 12-18 months, in our view.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley
Research as only a single factor in making their investment

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
Connectivity has become the bottleneck, and NPO is a potential solution. We
observed growing discussion and interest in NPO at the conference.
October 14, 2026 08:47 AM GMT

## Document 121: 261015_ml_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261015_ml_MTK.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

MediaTek

ASIC business expanding into Bytedance; 
potential high-end mobile gains in ‘27

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 6,200 TWD | Price: 4,475 TWD

Bytedance expanding ASIC TAM beyond Google/xAI 
Bloomberg reported on October 15th that Bytedance is close to an agreement to work 
with Mediatek on the ASIC design and turnkey service support following its collaboration 
with Broadcom and Qualcomm. This is consistent with our update in September that 
Mediatek could co-develop an AI inference chipset with Bytedance from 1H27 for tape 
out in 1H28 and see contribution from production in early ’29. Similar to Mediatek and 
xAI’s collaboration, we believe Bytedance would need the support on sourcing 
components (e.g. HBM and substrate) in addition to wafer manufacturing/packaging, 
lifting the dollar content vs. Google’s TPUs. Assuming 1mn unit demand annually & 
US$20-25k ASPs including advanced logic dies and HBM, the project could contribute 
US$20-25bn to Mediatek’s sales in ’29 (vs. BofA sales expectation on Mediatek  of 
US$82bn in ’28 with US$45bn from Google TPUv8/v9 and US$9bn from xAI’s 1-2 
quarter contribution). 
A modest tailwind in mobile as it gains share at Samsung 
The mobile chipset builds from Mediatek and Qualcomm in 2H26 are tracking below 
seasonal in 2H26 as the end demand is impacted by the elevated memory cost. While 
the market outlook looms into ’27 (vs. -25% Android unit demand in ’26), we expect 
Mediatek’s mobile business to fare better especially in the high-end segment where it 
could gain unit share by penetrating into Samsung’s flagship smartphones historically 
dominated by Qualcomm and Samsung’s own SoCs. Factoring 30% share in Samsung’s 
high-end mobile shipment of 30-35mn annual units and US$200+ chipset ASPs, it could 
be ~20% accretive to Mediatek’s mobile sales and ~5% of its total business from 2H27. 
EPS and valuation downside limited after recent pullback 
We maintain our Buy rating & NT$6,200 PO on 24x 2H27-1H28E P/E (implied 30x ASIC 
EPS + 10x merchant EPS). The share has been dragged by TPU competition concern in 
the near term but could be lifted by expanding ASIC projects and high-end mobile gains.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
MediaTek is a fabless IC design company supplying to 
wireless communications and digital consumer markets. 
Before taking leadership in mobile SoC area, it had built 
solid ground in the TV SoC and optical disk drive markets. 
The firm invests heavily in R&D to seek better positioning 
in the 5G cycle, and has a complete offering including ASIC, 
Wi-Fi, PMIC etc. to address applications beyond 
smartphone, such as IoT and auto.

Investment Rationale 
We have Buy rating on MediaTek as we think MediaTek is 
ready and has the strength to brace for the upcoming 
proliferation of IoT devices and opportunities in growing 
markets like ASIC, ARM-based PC, auto, and etc., and we 
believe its solid balance sheet and cashflow could provide 
downside support to the share price. Meanwhile, we expect 
5G smartphone penetration to be a multi-year theme for 
the company, with its better competitive landscape in 
duopoly 5G era and an improved margin profile.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
MediaTek (MDTKF) 
Our PO of NT$6,200 is based on 24x 2H27-1H28 P/E. which is towards the higher end 
of its historical P/E range of 8-25x, as we believe the company's broadening ASIC 
pipeline could drive an earnings upgrade cycle.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) better than expected smartphone demand, especially in 
China, (2) a better pricing in mobile communication segment thanks to alleviated 
competition and rising exposure to high-end/flagship market, and (3) an even more 
superior investment return on non-op side to uplift its net cash position. (4) Unexpected 
collaboration with global technology leaders leads to higher than expected 
margins/profits

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) weaker-than-expected end-market situation in 
consumer-related smartphone/TV space, (2) macro challenges including inflation/policy-
related pressures, (3) rising competition from existing players and/or newcomers in 
smartphone/TV SoC (system on chip) space, and (4) ASP/margin pressure caused by each 
or all of the above and/or rising foundry costs. (4) Bidding process for Meta Inference 
ASIC is slower than expected.

Greater China Technology Hardware

Industry View In-Line
At the rack level, Maia 300 is the key incremental story, while
Nvidia/AMD exhibits offered limited new system-level hardware.
Nvidia’s MGX component upgrades are the main supply-chain
watch points, although PTFE and next-gen interconnect and
cooling connector (ZQD) adoption remains uncertain.

Nvidia/AMD –  focus on ramps rather than new platforms. Most ODM displays
were similar to Computex Taipei (early June), so timing of ramp is key. For Nvidia,
VR200 racks have started a small-volume ramp, with the Nvidia Groq 3 LPX rack
following in late 2026/early 2027 and standalone Vera CPU racks in late 1Q27/early
2Q27, based on our checks. For AMD, Helios remained largely unchanged with
recent checks, with racks ramping in 1Q27, with pilot-build observations pointing to

Shennan Circuits for EAM PCBs and Fositek/Parker for quick disconnects.
Maia 300 -  a larger in-house inference opportunity. Microsoft plans production
deployments in 2027 as it scales Maia toward multi-gigawatt deployments. Each
rack integrates 96 accelerators within 167 kW. Based on that, each gigawatt of
deployment would imply 5 to 5.5K racks, and we believe Hon Hai and Wiwynn could
be the ODM partners, while our pilot-build observations point to ISU Petasys for the

accelerator PCB, AVC for fans, and Fositek/Staubli for quick disconnects.
PTFE - technical upside, but no confirmed timing of adoption. Nvidia is evaluating
PTFE for Rubin Ultra on Oberon following the Kyber delay and showcased its
NVLink PTFE PCB with seven suppliers (though we think there are suppliers missing
from this list). Better signal performance and reduced reliance on Q glass are
potential benefits, but processing complexity, higher costs and yield/qualification

risks remain hurdles to commercial adoption.
Ultra Dense Interconnect - next-generation interconnect opportunity for FIT.
Nvidia showcased six suppliers for this next-generation interconnect, which is
intended to replace Amphenol’s current HD2 connector in midplane and cable
cartridge applications. However, we believe adoption is still about two years away. In
the meantime, the supply chain is working to improve yields and bring ASPs below
US$100, with pogo-pin supply currently a bottleneck. Our checks identify FIT as the

key design partner for Kyber’s planned board-to-board connector, a position that
bodes well for its supply share once the product ramps.
ZQD - higher flow density and an expected ASP premium. Nvidia’s new ZQD
targets higher flow rates per unit area and was displayed with 14 suppliers.
Although the supply chain is working to improve yields and lower costs, greater
design complexity should keep ASPs above current QD pricing of US$20–30.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

### Page 2

M
Idea
2
Major Takeaways from OCP Summit 2026
ODM/OEM: Almost all the ODM/OEMs had a booth at OCP, but during our visit, we feel
most of the products were largely similar to what was showcased at Computex, such as
the Vera Rubin NVL72 rack, the LPX tray, the Vera standalone tray. So net, we think from a
hardware perspective, there was nothing new on Nvidia or AMD. We believe the Nvidia
VR200 rack is already starting ramp in small volume, with the LPX rack starting to ramp

later this year or early next year, and the Vera standalone CPU rack ramping late 1Q27 or
early 2Q27.
AMD: Wiwynn, Meta, and AMD each showcased the Helios rack, which was unchanged
from last year’s OCP display with one exception: this year’s version featured the actual
compute tray in place of last year’s dummy tray, allowing a closer look. That said, the
compute tray itself was not new to us, as we had already seen it at OCP Taiwan in August.
• Based on our observations at the OCP Summit, we believe Shennan Circuits is one

of the pilot build PCB suppliers for the MI455 Helios EAM PCB.
• Based on our observations at the OCP Summit, we believe Fositek (MQD02) and
Parker (UQD04) are one of the pilot build quick disconnect connector suppliers for
the MI455 Helios compute tray.
• HPE displayed the Helios switch tray at the OCP Summit. We believe the other
supplier of the Helios switch trays is Celestica.
Microsoft: Maia 300 represents a major expansion of Microsoft’s in-house inference

infrastructure. Announced on October 12, 2026, with production deployments planned for
2027, it forms part of Microsoft’s commitment to scale the Maia family to multi-gigawatt
deployments and make it the primary inference platform for its products and services. The
design targets long-context reasoning, conversational AI, and multi-step agentic
workloads, with an emphasis on improving inference economics through coordinated
silicon, software, and system design.

Maia 300 rack integrates 96 accelerators within a 167 kW power envelope, providing
approximately 27.6 TB of aggregate HBM capacity. There are 4 accelerators per tray,
implying 24 compute trays per rack, including six high-radix switch ASICs that are
integrated into three liquid-cooled switch trays, providing the rack-local connectivity
required by all 96 accelerators. Microsoft projects 40% more tokens per megawatt than
leading GPU-based systems and up to 7× Maia 200’s rack-level token throughput, though

this takes into account that the Maia 300 rack  includes twice as many accelerators per
rack.
• The Official Microsoft Blog A two-tier Ethernet scale-up network connects up to
128 racks, or 12,288 accelerators, while liquid cooling removes more than 80% of
rack heat. Each compute tray includes 8x fans  (Exhibit 3 ), and based on our
observations at the OCP Summit, we believe AVC is one of the pilot build fan
suppliers for the sample compute tray.

• Based on our observations at the OCP Summit, we believe ISU Petasys is one of
the pilot build PCB suppliers for Microsoft’s Maia 300 AI accelerator.
• Based on our observations at the OCP Summit, we believe Fositek and Staubli are
pilot build MQD03 (quick disconnect connector) suppliers for the compute tray.

### Page 3

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 3
• We believe Hon Hai and Wiwynn could be ODM partners for these racks.
• With each rack being 167 kW of power, one gigawatt of deployment would imply 5-
5.5K racks, assuming a PUE of 1.1-1.2.

Exhibit 3: Maia 300 server compute tray
Source: Microsoft.
Nvidia MGX Ecosystem
PTFE: We previously wrote that our supply chain checks suggest that Nvidia had initially
planned to adopt PTFE CCL for the very high-layer-count midplane in its Kyber
architecture. With Kyber now delayed, and Rubin Ultra no longer expected to use that
architecture, as previously discussed, the focus has shifted back to the Oberon
architecture. Nvidia is again evaluating PTFE for Rubin Ultra on Oberon, and at this year’s

OCP Summit, Nvidia showcased its NVLink PTFE PCB, with a total of 7 suppliers, including
VGT, TTM, Taiflex, SYTECH, Shennan Circuits, Kinwong, and Flexium (Exhibit 4  & Exhibit
5 ). However, our checks indicate that actual adoption for Rubin Ultra remains uncertain.
However, we find the supplier list puzzling. Our checks suggest that WUS PCB and
Unimicron are also working on this solution, and that Flexium, which has collaborated
with other PCB suppliers, may handle only a few process steps. Our checks also indicate

that Shennan Circuits, WUS PCB and VGT are now the leading PCB makers for this
solution, with relatively higher yields.
Advantages of PTFE
• Best signal performance: the lowest Dk and Df of the mainstream options, below
even standard M9-class CCLs. This matters most where insertion-loss budgets are
tightest, i.e. the highest speeds and highest layer counts.
• Stable electricals: the low Dk/Df hold up across frequency and temperature, and
PTFE absorbs very little moisture, so performance is stable in use.

• Less dependence on Q glass: because PTFE’s intrinsic dielectric constant is already
very low, reaching the target Dk requires less (and in some hybrid constructions,
none) of the expensive, supply-constrained Q glass. That is both a cost lever and a
supply-security benefit.
Disadvantages of PTFE

### Page 5

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 5
• Mechanically soft: low modulus makes it difficult to handle and to hold to
tolerance.
• Difficult lamination: PTFE does not flow like a thermoset, so bonding needs either
very high temperatures or lower-melting fluoropolymer (such as PFA
(polyfluoroalkoxy))/thermoset bond layers; its high, non-linear CTE stresses
copper adhesion and complicates layer-to-layer registration, and pressing typically
requires vacuum or an inert atmosphere with longer cycles.

• Difficult drilling: the soft, gummy resin tends to smear and can tear, and any
ceramic filler is abrasive on tooling. Also, the hole wall will not accept electroless
copper without an added activation step (sodium etch or plasma) on top of normal
desmear.
• Higher cost and qualification risk: materials and fabrication are both more
expensive, the qualified supply base is narrower, and yields at very high layer
counts carry risk.
Exhibit 4: Nvidia's NVLink PTFE Board

Source: Nvidia, Morgan Stanley Research.

Ultra Dense Interconnect: Currently, Nvidia's NVL72 racks are using Amphenol's Paladin
HD2 connectors for VR200 NVL72 midplanes and cable cartridges. Our previous checks
done around Computex 2026 in June, indicate ASP could reach US$70-90+ per set. For
the next-generation Kyber racks, our checks have previously indicated that a new midplane
connector called "Voronoi" will be introduced, and FIT is the key design partner for this

new board-to-board connector. At this year’s OCP Summit, Nvidia showcased a new
connector it calls the “Ultra Dense Interconnect”, with six suppliers in total. This was our
first in-person look at the design. We believe adoption is still two years out, and the
supply chain is currently focused on improving yields and optimizing costs to bring the
ASP below US$100. Our checks suggest that pogo pin supply is currently a bottleneck for
this connector.

### Page 7

Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 7
Exhibit 6: Nvidia next-generation Ultra Dense Interconnect
Source: Nvidia, Morgan Stanley Research.
ZQD: At COMPUTEX in Taipei earlier this year, we noted that our checks indicated Nvidia
was planning to introduce a new quick disconnect (QD) design for next-generation server
racks to achieve higher flow rates per unit area as cooling requirements rise. At this year’s
OCP Summit, Nvidia showcased that design, called ZQD, with 14 suppliers in total. Our

checks indicate the supply chain is also working to improve yields and optimize costs to
bring ZQD ASPs down. However, given the greater complexity of the design, we expect
ZQD ASPs to remain above current QD pricing of US$20–30 per set.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

OSAT 展望樂觀，SOIC 機台具技術優勢 
 2027 主要成長動能仍為 OSAT:2026 年弘塑產能擴充一倍 ，1Q26 已
經完成擴建 ，3Q26 新產能已經滿載。 外包部分 ， 目前外包比例33%，
未來佔比會下降，目前外包月產能一個月 5-6 台，因為外包產能受
限，同業也在搶外包產能。2027 主要成長動能仍為 OSAT，今年晶
圓代工客戶營收高於 OSAT，2027 年 OSAT 營收超過晶圓代工客戶，
SOIC 機台去年小量，今年量增，2027 跟 2026 差不多略增，2028
才會明顯大增，主要還是跟隨晶圓代工客戶 SOIC 擴廠進度。 
 2028-2029 年 PLP 機台出貨量會很明顯放大:PLP 及 CoPoS 設備機
台單價很高，初估有機會是 CoWoS 翻倍，PLP 進度比較快，PLP
規格弘塑 310*310、510*515、600*600 都有，再大到 700 以上就不
做了，晶圓代工客戶規格是 310*310 但目前進度比較慢，還在實驗
線階段。力成 510*515 進度最快，4Q26 會拉第一批機台，2027 隨
力成購置的友達廠進度持續拉機台，2028-2029 年 PLP 機台出貨量
會很明顯放大。 
 維持買進評等，目標價 3000 元：基本面無變化，預估數字也沒做調
整，故維持買進評等 ，預估 2026 年 EPS 64.81 元，2027 年 EPS 93.97
元，目標價 3000 元。(2027 PER 30x)

弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商 
 弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商 
弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商，主要利基產品為半導體後段濕製程設備，並延
伸相關化學品、檢測設備代理及軟體解決方案。1H26 產品結構可區分為機台檢
測產品(65%)、化學產品(20%)、代理設備(15%)。2013 年合併化學品公司添鴻
(CLC)、2018 年底合併通路商佳霖(ClC)、併購資訊系統廠太引(Tyne)，投資美
國 X-Ray 檢測公司 SVXR。 客戶為國內半導體 、 光電 、記憶體及面板產業大廠，
包括台積電、矽品、日月光、力成、星科金朋、精材、聯電、茂矽、采鈺、穏
懋、中芯、宏達等。

2Q26 毛 利率 39.37%， 低於預
期，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元 
 2Q26 毛利率 39.37%，低於預期，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元 
弘塑 2Q26 EPS 11.18 元，低於元富預估的 12.34 元，獲利達成率 91%。本業
來看，2Q26 營收 17.27 億元，+8%QOQ，+6%YOY，2Q26 毛利率 39.37%，
較 1Q26 回升 5.1 個百分點，然仍低於預估的 41%，主要是外包成本及急單增
加影響部分毛利率，預期 3Q26 毛利率持續向上改善，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元。

弘塑 3Q26 營收 22.67 億元，+31.3%QOQ，+51.8%YOY，營收達成率 98%，
略低於預期，預估 3Q26 毛利率 41%，預估 3Q26 EPS 16.47 元。

營運逐季成長，OSA T展望樂觀

營運逐季成長，OSA T展望樂觀 
2026 年弘塑擴產一倍 ，1Q26 已經完成擴建 ，3Q26 新產能已經滿載。 外包部分 ，
目前外包比例 33%，未來佔比會下降，目前外包月產能一個月 5-6 台，因為外
包產能受限 ， 同業也在搶外包產能 。2027 年擴產再 50-100%， 以租用廠房為主。
2027 主要成長動能還是 OSAT，今年晶圓代工客戶營收高於 OSAT，2027 年
OSAT 營收超過晶圓代工客戶，SOIC 機台去年小量，今年量增，2027 跟 2026
差不多略增 ，2028 才會明顯大增 ， 主要還是跟隨晶圓代工客戶 SOIC 擴廠進度，
SOIC 機台種類 6-7 種。

## Document 183: 9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象

Source PDF: all report pdf/9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 7 日(水)

1/3 
9 月会合以降に日銀を取り巻く状況が変わった印象  
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・時事通信は10月６日付で「日銀が『基調的な物価上昇率』について、２％程度に到達したと
判断する方向で検討に入ることが５日、分かった」と報じた。記事内には10月29・30日開催
の金融政策決定会合で取りまとめる展望レポートで、そうした認識が記述されるとしてい
る。

・筆者はこの報道が現実になる可能性が高いと判断している。日銀は予てから基調的物価上昇
率が２％に到達する時期を「2026年度後半から2027年度」と予想してきたので（2026年度後
半にあたる）10月にそうした認識を示さないと、ハト派的な印象を与えてしまうことにな
る。

・既に日銀の政策態度は基調的物価上昇率を２％に「引き上げる」段階から、２％以上に「上
振れさせない」段階に移行している。局面が変化した理由としては、やはり原油高とそれに
対する企業の価格設定行動の積極化が大きいだろう。従来、原油高は企業収益を圧迫するこ
とで、賃上げを阻害し、個人消費の停滞に繋がってきた。そうした環境下で内需は拡大しな
いため、原油高は短期的にインフレ要因となっても、やや長い目でみるとデフレ的な色彩を
帯びていた。もっとも、2026年春先以降の原油高局面では、政府の手厚い支援もあり、企業
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
は価格転嫁を積極的に進めた。企業物価指数は４月に前月比＋2.8％と即座に上昇し、前年比
伸び率は８月時点でも＋7.6％と高止まりしている。日銀短観（６月および９月調査）では１
年後の販売価格見通しが急伸した。こうした早期かつ大幅な価格転嫁の進展によって、企業
収益は傷みにくくなっている。このことは、名目賃金と名目消費が伸びやすい構図にあるこ
とを浮き彫りにしている。

・日銀が、基調的物価上昇率が２％に到達したとの認識を示すのであれば、それは利上げペー
スの加速を自ら正当化することになる。さすがに10月の利上げはビハインド・ザ・カーブに
陥ったことを認めたと市場参加者に受け止められる恐れがあるため、見送られると予想する
が、（前回利上げから約３ヶ月が経過する）12月会合では利上げが決定されるだろう。そう
なれば、３～４ヶ月に一度の利上げが標準ペースとなることが市場参加者に広く意識される
だろう。

・利上げを巡っては、経済指標に加えて「政府との距離感」を考慮する必要があり、予想は難
儀であった。しかしながら、ここ数週間で情勢は変化したように感じる。９月25日に片山さ
つき財務相が「官邸とも相談の上、私から初めて申し上げるが、先日の日米首脳会談では、
為替について、トランプ大統領から円安への懸念が示された」と発言したことを皮切りに、
同日には城内経済再生相が「アベノミクス的な意味でのリフレーション政策は、もうそうい
う局面は終わっている」とした。また10月５日に公表された９月30日の経済財政諮問会議の
議事要旨では、民間議員である若田部昌澄氏が「デフレではない状況になりつつある現状で
リフレ政策が終焉を迎えているというのは当然のことだ」との見解を示し、さらに９月の金
融政策決定会合で反対票を投じた佐藤綾野日銀政策委員は６日までに共同通信の単独インタ

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
ビューに応じて「（政策金利を）段階的に調整していく方針には賛成している」とした。９
月18日会合における「主な意見」で内閣府の出席者（おそらく城内大臣）が利上げに消極的
と読める発言をしていたことと整合しないが、それは9月18日から25日の間に政府の態度が変
化した可能性が指摘できる。

・10月会合までに円安が進む、あるいは原油高等の要因によってＦｅｄが利上げに踏み切る事
態となれば、日銀も追随する可能性があるものの、そうした状況に至らなければ、10月は
「既往の利上げ効果を見極めたい」などとして利上げを見送るだろう。

・なお、これらに鑑みて、筆者は2027年７月の審議委員人事が中立ないしはタカ派的な人選に
なると予想する。高田委員と田村委員に考え方の近い人物に白羽の矢が立ち、リフレ派が送
り込まれることはないと考えている。利上げに積極的な２名の後任人事にリフレ派と目され
る人物が浮上した場合、金融市場では長期金利上昇や円安の反応が予想される。

## Document 188: 9月经济数据跟踪：经济寻底接近尾声，政策托举四季度增速

Source PDF: all report pdf/9月经济数据跟踪：经济寻底接近尾声，政策托举四季度增速.pdf
Report source: 万联证券
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

[Table_RightTitle] 
宏观研究|宏观跟踪报告

[Table_Title] 
经济寻底接近尾声，政策托举四季度增速

[Table_ReportType] 
——9 月经济数据跟踪

[Table_ReportDate] 2026 年 10 月 19 日

[T able_Summary] ⚫ 事件：2026 年Q3,GDP 不变价同比4.5%，环比1.2%。工业增加值
同比由5.2%上行至5.5%，固投累计同比持平于-7.2%，其中制造
业、基建、房地产投资分别由-2.3%、-4%、-19.9%变动为-2.1%、
-4.2%、-20.6%，社零当月同比由0.4%上行至1.1%。 
⚫ 三季度GDP 略高于市场预期，全年经济目标有望达成。2026 年三
季度GDP 不变价同比增速为4.5%，高于市场预期的4.4%。第二、
三产业对GDP 同比拉动作用边际放缓，第一产业对GDP 同比拉动
作用加强。工业产能利用率环比上行，采矿业整体小幅回升，制
造业整体小幅回落，行业分化明显。 
⚫ 工业生产季节性回升，出口拉动作用走强，高技术产业生产高位
边际放缓。工业企业出口交货值大幅拉升，出口给部分行业生产
带来较强拉动。采矿业生产持续修复，石油开采业为主要拉动，
煤炭产量跌幅收窄。制造业生产整体小幅回落，上游制造业中，
非金属矿、黑色金属冶炼行业生产上行，极端天气影响消退，施
工旺季带来需求回补，政策性金融工具加速落地，带来行业边际
改善。化工在地缘政策不确定性影响下，企业补库需求提升，行
业生产上行，有色金属冶炼及压延工业、橡胶和塑料制品业在价
格走高等影响下，生产同比回落。中游制造业表现分化，整体仍
处高位，消费品制造业整体回落。下游制造业中，汽车、运输设
备、电子设备制造同比均回落，新质生产力生产边际放缓，新旧
动能分化收敛。 
⚫ 服务业生产指数回升，生产性服务表现仍强。“双节”给服务业
带来一定提振，服务业活动预期指数持平。商务活动指数上行，
信息传输、软件和信息技术服务业与租赁和商务服务业同比小幅
上行，租赁和运输业同比均有所修复。 
⚫ 投资止跌企稳，资金投放和落地稳步推进。随着稳增长政策逐步
落地，固定资产投资逐步止跌企稳。设备购置在政策推动下持续
上行，建安工程继续下跌，新开工偏弱，投资项目不足的影响仍
存。固投资金来源来看，今年以来债券累计同比维持高位，政府
专项债用于项目建设比重明显提升，预算内资金同比跌幅有所收
窄，项目资金逐步落地。 
⚫ 地产投资持续下滑，销售依然偏弱。地产新开工、竣工跌幅加
深，施工增速持平。地产销售“金九”行情表现偏弱，二手房销
售好于新房。一线城市和部分二线城市新房价格环比上行，三线
城市价格依然偏弱，二手房价格同比多数下跌，房价依然有待企
稳。9 月 29 日居民购房贷款贴息政策公布，有望给刚需带来一定
利好，政策效果仍有待观望。房企资金来源持续收紧，销售回款
和融资环境依然承压，房企拿地偏谨慎。四季度土地供应总量收
缩的特征预期持续，结构逐步改善。 
[Table_Authors] 分析师: 于天旭 
执业证书编号： S0270522110001 
电话： 17717422697 
邮箱： yutx@wlzq.com.cn

⚫ 基建投资持续放缓，制造业投资反弹上行，财政资金有望给四季
度增速带来支撑。三季度以来财政资金投放有所提速，但落地进
程依然偏慢，基建下行斜率明显放缓。随着8000 亿元政策性金融
工具、政府专项债和地方政府债务结存限额在四季度投放并落
地，“六张网”建设进程有望提速，四季度基建将发挥“压舱
石”的作用。制造业投资上行部分对冲了地产、基建下行的压
力，高技术产业增速边际放缓。大规模设备更新政策、出口强韧
性，以及稳增长政策逐步落地给制造业带来拉动。 
⚫ 消费增速修复， “双节”带来提振，结构表现分化。服务消费好
转，餐饮增速上行，线上消费小幅放缓。居民实际可支配收入边
际上行，人均消费支出持平，居民消费信心窄幅震荡，内需整体
依然偏弱。可选消费结构分化，汽车为主要拖累，地产下游消费
偏弱。金银珠宝类本月跌幅收窄最为显著，通讯器材类消费持续
走高，假期出行需求提振下，石油及制品类跌幅收窄。必选消费
整体表现稳健，粮油、食品类、日用品类上行幅度较大。 
⚫ 四季度政策将发挥托底作用，关注政策落地效果。9 月我国经济
外需拉动、生产强、内需弱的格局持续，但新旧经济分化程度边
际收敛。9 月稳增长政策逐步加码，政府债发行提速，在政策扶
持下，四季度经济有望缓步回升，但仍需关注财政资金向实物工
作量的转化效率，预期全年经济节奏呈现“U 型”走势。 
⚫ 风险因素：地缘政治风险发展超预期，居民购房持续低迷，我国
政策出台和落地不及预期

事件 
2026年三季度，GDP不变价同比增速4.5%， 季调环比增长1.2%， 前值0.9%。9月，工业
增加值当月同比增速上行0.3个百分点至5.5%，环比上涨0.61%，前值为0.54%。前三
季度，固定资产投资累计同比增速-7.2%，8月为-7.2%，其中，房地产开发投资累计
同比增速回落0.7个百分点至-20.6%，基建投资累计同比增速回落 0.2个百分点至-
4.2%， 制造业投资增速累计同比上行0.2个百分点至-2.1%。 社会消费品零售总额同比
增速由上月的0.4%上行至1.1%，

1    三季度经济增速略高于市场预期 
三季度GDP增速略高于市场预期，外需强劲拉动生产为主要支撑，全年经济目标有望
达成。2026年三季度GDP不变价同比增速为4.5%，市场预期为4.4%，一、二季度同比
增速分别为5%、4.3%；三季度环比增速为1.2%， 高于前值0.9%， 前三季度累计同比为
4.6%。分产业看，第一、二、三产业累计同比增速分别由上半年的3.7%、3.9%、5.2%
变动为3.7%、3.8%、5.2%，仅第二产业同比增速小幅放缓。第二、三产业对GDP同比
拉动作用边际放缓，第一产业对GDP同比拉动作用加强。二季度以来，地缘政治扰动
给全球经济、 物价带来较大风险， 我国出口保持强劲，给工业生产带来拉动， 为经济
重要支撑。 原油等大宗商品涨价压力加大， 输入性通胀被动抬升，我国工业品价格上
涨，CPI总体表现温和。 
工业产能利用率环比上行，行业分化明显。工业产能利用率小幅上行至73.1%，二季
度为73%。分行业来看，采矿业整体小幅回升，但仍低于去年同期，其中煤炭开采和
洗选环比上行幅度较大， 石油和天然气开采业小幅回落。电力、 热力、 燃气及水生产
和供应业环比回升。制造业整体小幅回落， 上游制造业中， 非金属矿物制品、 化学原
料及化学制品业产能利用率环比修复， 有色、 黑色金属冶炼及压延产能利用率同环比
下滑。中游专用设备制造产能利用率环比上行，同比高于去年同期， 通用设备制造环
比放缓，但仍处于高位，电气机械和器材制造环比上行明显。下游制造业表现分化，
计算机、 通信和其他电子设备产能利用率由78.7%上行至80.2%， 领跑其他行业； 汽车
制造业同、环比下跌幅度较大，医药制造业也有所回落。

2    工业生产回升，出口拉动作用较强 
季末月生产季节性回升， 出口拉动作用走强， 采矿业持续修复， 高技术产业生产高位
边际放缓。9月， 工业增加值当月同比增速上行0.3个百分点至5.5%，与市场预期的5.5%
一致，前三季度累计同比增速为5.3%。9月环比上涨0.61%，前值为0.54%。发电量同
比由上月的-0.8%回落至-1.2%。工业企业出口交货值当月同比由上月的11.1%上行至
17%，规模以上工业企业产销率当月同比由-0.1%下行为-0.2%。分三大门类看，9月，
采矿业同比上行2.7个百分点至1.3%，石油和天然气开采业本月同比上行幅度相对最
大，原煤产量同比跌幅收窄，天然原油产量同比上行，天然气产量同比由正转负。制
造业同比由上月的6.1%回落至6%； 高技术产业同比由上月的16.7%回落至15.3%。 电力、
燃气及水的生产和供应业同比上行0.7个百分点至5.6%。

图表3：采矿业持续修复，高技术产业高位放缓（%） 图表4：工业企业出口交货值增速攀升（亿元，%）

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

化工、 黑色金属行业生产上行， 下游制造业生产整体放缓， 消费品制造业整体回落。
细分行业来看， 上游制造业中，化学原料及化学制品制造业增速持续上行， 中东地缘
政治给供应链和原料价格带来较大扰动， 不确定性下企业补库需求提升， 乙烯、 化纤
产量上行。 非金属矿物制品、黑色金属冶炼及压延工业同比修复上行， 钢材、 水泥产
量同比跌幅收窄，极端天气影响消退后， 施工旺季带来需求回补， 政策性金融工具加
速落地， 叠加原材料成本上涨带来行业边际改善。 有色金属冶炼及压延工业、橡胶和
塑料制品业同比回落， 价格高位给生产带来一定抑制。中游制造业表现分化， 整体仍
处高位， 其中，通用设备、电气机械及器材制造业同比回落，专用设备制造业同比上
行。下游制造业中，汽车制造业同比回落2个百分点至6.7%，下滑幅度较大，高基数
带来一定影响；铁路、船舶、航空航天和其他运输设备制造业同比回落1.7个百分点
至11.7%，增速持续放缓。计算机、通信和其他电子设备制造业同比回落，工业机器
人产量同比下滑幅度较大，集成电路产量同比下行，微型电子计算机产量跌幅收窄。
消费品制造业中，医药制造业同比回落0.3 个百分点至 4.9% ， 农副食品加工业、食品
制造业生产增速放缓，纺织业同比小幅上行。

服务业生产指数回升， “双节”带来一定提振，商务活动也有所修复，生产性服务表
现仍强，服务业活动预期指数持平。服务业生产指数9月当月同比为4.8%， 较前值4.1%
显著上行， 中秋节和十一长假给服务业带来一定提振， 前三季度累计同比为4.7%，与

受到信心因素制约。 商务活动指数当月同比由49.3%上行至50.2%，信息传输、 软件和
信息技术服务业与租赁和商务服务业同比小幅上行，租赁和运输业同比均有所修复，

3    投资：地产、基建延续磨底，制造业增速边际修复 
投资增速止跌企稳，资金投放和落地稳步推进，设备购置在政策推动下持续上行， 建
安工程继续下跌。2026年前三季度， 固定资产投资累计同比增速-7.2%，8月为-7.2%，
高于市场预期的-7.5%。固投季调环比增速为0.07%， 前值为-0.55%。随着稳增长政策
逐步落地， 固定资产投资逐步止跌企稳。其中， 房地产开发投资累计同比增速继续回
落0.7个百分点至-20.6%；基建累计同比增速回落0.2个百分点至-4.2%，制造业累计
同比增速上行0.2个百分点至-2.1%，民间固定资产投资回落0.3个百分点至-10.4%。
设备工器具购置同比增速由9.3%上行至9.7%，设备更新政策效果持续显现；建筑安装
工程同比由-9.8%回落为-10%，新开工偏弱，投资项目不足的影响仍存。第二产业投
资增速上行至主要支撑， 第三产业投资增速小幅回落， 第一产业下滑幅度较大。固投
资金来源来看，今年以来债券累计同比维持高位， 政府专项债用于项目建设比重明显
提升，预算内资金同比跌幅有所收窄，项目资金逐步落地。

3.1  房地产投资持续下滑，销售依然偏弱 
房地产投资增速持续下探，新开工、竣工跌幅加深。2026年9月，房地产投资累计同
比增速继续回落0.7个百分点至-20.6%，具体来看，房屋新开工面积累计同比增速回
落0.1个百分点至-24.9%，房屋施工面积累计同比持平于上月的-12.8%，竣工增速由
-23.7%回落为-25.1%。 
地产销售“金九”行情表现偏弱，二手房销售好于新房。9月，商品房销售面积累计
同比由-12.1%继续下探至-13.2%， 商品房销售额由-13%下跌为-13.9%， 房地产销售 “量”
的跌幅相对 “价” 更大。从70城房价数据来看， 一线城市新房和二手房销售价格环比
上涨， 二手房价格同比多数下跌，仅上海同比上行。 部分二线城市新房销售价格环比
上行， 三线城市新房、 二手房价格同环比持续放缓， 房价依然有待企稳。二手房交易
网签面积累计同比由10.6%小幅上行至10.7%， 二手房销售好于新房的格局持续， 但二
手房交易景气难以传导至房企回款与投资。商品房待售面积累计同比由-1.1%下跌至
-1.5%， 其中住宅累计同比由-0.5% 下行至 - 0.7% ， 地产库存持续去化。 8 月地产政策陆
续出台， 北京、 上海等核心城市购房调节进一步放松。 地产直至竣工验收具备交付条
件后方可收取购房资金和按揭贷款，优化了居民的购房环境，但从9月数据来看提振
效果相对有限。9月29日居民购房贷款贴息政策公布， 政策在10月1日期实施， 有望给

房企资金来源持续收紧， 销售回款和融资环境依然承压。房地产开发资金来源增速由
-21%回落为-21.6%。其中，个人按揭贷款跌幅小幅收窄，二手房交易活跃带来支撑，
定金及预收款增速与上月持平， 地产 “金九” 行情偏弱， 地产销售回款受限。 国内贷
款跌幅小幅修复上行， 自筹资金增速进一步下滑，地产行业信贷投放保持审慎， 房企
资金来源持续承压。 
房企拿地同比持续回落， 去库背景下， 房企依然偏谨慎。9月，100大中城市成交土地
占地面积环比季节性上行， 但同比由-16.1%继续小幅下调至-16.8%，房企拿地依然集
中在一线城市与核心城市的优质地块。房地产仍处去库进程当中，预售资金制度改革
影响将逐步显现， 房企资金周转受到约束， 叠加销售端和资金来源的限制，四季度土
地供应总量收缩的特征预期持续，结构逐步改善。

3.2  基建增速持续放缓，财政资金落地有望给行业带来托举

显放缓， 但落地进程依然偏慢。 随着8000亿元政策性金融工具、 政府专项债和地方政
府债务结存限额在四季度投放并落地， “六张网” 建设进程有望提速， 四季度基建将

发挥“压舱石”的作用。 
基建分项整体表现偏弱， 公共设施管理业下滑幅度较大。从细分项来看，水利、 环境
和公共设施管理业跌幅进一步加深， 且下滑幅度最大。交通运输、 仓储和邮政业增速
持续下探， 但下行速度边际放缓。 电力、 热力、 燃气及水的生产和供应业增速小幅修
复上行，但仍为负增，随着高基数效应减弱，增速有望边际修复。

3.3  制造业投资反弹上行，高技术产业增速放缓 
制造业投资反弹上行，高技术产业增速边际放缓，出口和政策为主要拉动。9月，制
造业累计同比增速上行0.2个百分点至-2.1%，部分对冲了地产、 基建投资下行的压力，
民间固定资产投资回落0.3个百分点至-10.4%，依然表现偏弱；高技术产业累计增速
由5.2%回落至4.9%，设备工器具购置同比增速由9.3%上行至9.7%。 大规模设备更新政
策、 出口强韧性， 以及稳增长政策逐步落地给制造业带来拉动，上游资源品、 关键材
料等自主补库需求提高，也给制造业带来支撑。四季度随着财政资金投放陆续落地，
给制造业的支撑效果有望逐步显现。

图表16：制造业投资反弹，与存货走势小幅收敛（%） 图表17：出口韧性给制造业投资带来支撑（%）

4    消费增速修复，结构表现分化 
消费增速修复上行， “双节”带来提振，服务消费好转，线上消费放缓，内需整体依
然偏弱。9月， 社会消费品零售总额同比增速由上月的0.4%上行至1.1%， 市场预期0.7%，
累计同比持平于上月的1.1%。其中， 除汽车以外的消费品零售增速由上月的2.5%上涨
为3.9%， 上行幅度大于整体； 餐饮同比由上月的1.1%上涨为2.1%； 限额以上企业零售
同比由上月的-3.7%上涨为-2.1%。实物商品网上零售累计同比由4.3%回落为3.8%，网
上商品和服务占社零比重高位小幅下滑。城镇消费同比由0.2%上行至0.8%， 农村消费
由1.6%回升至2.7%，两者差距再度走扩。 
居民实际可支配收入边际上行， 人均消费支出持平，居民消费信心依然偏弱。2026年
前三季度， 全国居民人均可支配收入名义累计同比增长5.3%， 上半年为5.2%；扣除价
格因素实际累计同比4.4%， 前值为4.2%； 居民人均消费支出累计同比持平于上半年的
3%。从数据更新至8月的消费者信心指数来看，居民消费信心和意愿均小幅上行，但
收入信心指数持续回落。居民存款缓步回落， 就业和收入压力下， 信心有待进一步修
复。 
图表18：居民存款高位缓步回落（%） 图表19：消费者信心指数上行，收入信心指数回落

资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所 资料来源：Wind，万联证券研究所

可选消费结构分化， 汽车为主要拖累， 地产下游消费偏弱， 必选消费表现稳健。可选
消费中，金银珠宝类本月跌幅收窄最为显著，黄金价格持续回落，高基数效应走弱，
给增速带来一定拉动。地缘政治影响下， 油价持续走高，在中秋和十一出行需求提振
下， 石油及制品类跌幅收窄。 汽车消费跌幅相对最大， 消费补贴和税费优惠政策退坡
带来一定影响。 地产下游行业中， 家电增速小幅放缓， 仍保持正增， 家具、 建材类跌
幅明显收窄。消费补贴对耐用品消费的透支作用逐步显现， 居民消费信心偏弱， 汽车、
地产后周期产品消费持续承压。 通讯器材类在高基数下， 增速持续走高， 新品发布周
期，内存价格大涨拉升了手机售价。必选消费增速均修复上行，其中粮油、食品类、
日用品类上行幅度较大。

5    失业率季节性回落，结构压力仍存 
失业率季节性回落， 农民工就业压力加大。9月，城镇调查失业率由5.3%下行至5.2%，
31个大城市城镇失业率也由5.3%下行至5.2%，外来农业户籍人口失业率持平于8月的
5.0%，BCI企业招工前瞻指数边际回落。9月就业人员平均工作时间由48.2小时/周上
行至48.3小时/周。从年龄结构来看，更新至8月的失业率数据中，16-24岁的年轻劳
动力失业率持续走高，25-29岁也有所上行，大学生毕业季带来的影响持续。随着大
学生就业逐步被吸纳，9月毕业季带来的影响季节性减弱。

6    总结与展望 
四季度政策将发挥托底作用，关注政策落地效果 。9月我国经济外需拉动、生产强、
内需弱的格局持续，但新旧经济分化程度边际收敛。9月稳增长政策逐步加码，政府
债发行提速， 在政策扶持下， 四季度经济有望缓步回升， 但仍需关注财政资金向实物
工作量的转化效率，预期全年经济节奏呈现“U型”走势。

7    风险提示 
地缘政治风险发展超预期，居民购房持续低迷，我国政策出台和落地不及预期。

## Document 196: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
여전히 가격 상승 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 95.8조원, 영업이익 75.1조원으로 예상한다. 
이전 전망 대비 감소한 수준이다. 원/달러 환율 영향이다. 영업이익률은 78.4%로 전 
분기 대비 2.1%p 상승할 전망이다. DRAM 매출액은 73.1조원, 영업이익 
59.2조원으로 추정한다. DRAM ASP는 이전 전망 대비 상향 조정했다. 일부 제품 
가격 인상을 반영한 영향이다. Bit Growth는 이전 전망 수준을 유지했다. ASP 
상향이 수익성 개선에 미친 영향은 크지 않을 전망이다. NAND 매출액은 22.4조원, 
영업이익은 15.9조원으로 예상한다. 
Global Top 수준 주주환원 규모/자사주 살 게 더 있다 
SK하이닉스는 2026년 8월 19일 주주환원에 대한 새로운 정책을 제시했다. FCF에서 
CAPEX를 제외한 금액의 50% 이상을 주주환원에 사용한다는게 핵심이다. 2026년 
기준으로 최소 90조원 수준일 것으로 추정한다. 자사주 중심으로 주주환원을 계획 
중이고 이미 집행 중인 40조원 이외에 4분기 중에 추가로 자사주 매입이 진행될 
것으로 예상한다. 배당도 일부 집행이 되겠지만 자사주가 중심이 될 전망이다. 
흔들리지 말아야 할 이유는 많다 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 모델의 성장으로 메모리에 
대한 수요가 지속적으로 높아지고 있고, 이에 비해 공급 증가는 제한적인 상황이 
상당기간 지속될 것으로 전망하기 때문이다. 이로 인해 현재 수준의 DRAM, NAND 
가격에 변동성은 낮고, 높은 수익성이 유지되면서 안정적 현금흐름 창출이 가능할 
것으로 전망한다. 현재 주가는 지나치게 저평가되어 있는 국면에 있다고 판단한다. 
목표주가는 12개월 예상 EPS 446,092원에 PER 9 배를 적용한 4,000,000원을 
유지한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 208: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다. 
목표주가 (하향) 2,000,000원

## Document 259: Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Chartpack _ Advanced economies_ Resilience to shock after shock (2026-10-06).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

By Advanced Economies Team
Advanced Economies:
Resilience to shock after shock
October 2026

### Page 2

• The global economy is in the midst of a growth cycle. As a result, GDP growth is more resilient to shocks than initially
anticipated.
• The figures for the second quarter bear this out, despite the sharp rise in oil prices, whose negative impact on growth is offset
by a series of reflationary forces.
• The driving force behind the current growth cycle is clear: artificial intelligence (AI).
• The world’s major economies are currently engaged in a broader push to expand their capital goods investment.

• While European and US households are experiencing more or less the same energy price shock, inflation and household
consumption remain higher in the US.
• Higher and above-target inflation calls for monetary tightening.
• Inflation is exceeding the target set by three of the four central banks examined in this publication (the US, the UK, the
Eurozone). Inflation is currently expected to peak in Q4 2026 or Q1 2027, but there is a significant risk that energy inflation will

spread further to other components of inflation in the future.
• Inflation can also be a sign of (too much) economic strength (in the US).
• Monetary policies also have to offset the inflationary effects of fiscal policy (particularly in Japan).
• The outbreak of the conflict in Iran acted as a catalyst: the ensuing rise in energy prices contributes to the recent increase in
long-term rates (which had been trending downwards at the start of the year) to levels close to those of 2002.

• The other issue is the high level of fiscal deficits (too high for a period of growth), reflecting different fiscal trajectories. In the
US, the UK, France and Germany the public debt-to-GDP ratio is increasing, while it has stabilized in Italy and decreased in
Spain and Japan.
• The rise in long-term interest rates will weigh on public finances. The fiscal arithmetic is simpler today than it will be in 2030.
By 2030, the effective rate will have risen and caught up with GDP growth.

For more information, please refer to our publication:
EcoPerspectives – Advanced Economies | 3rd quarter of 2026, as of September 24, 2026– Economic Research– BNP Paribas
Advanced economies: Resilience to shock after shock

## Document 295: FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus FEMO's Steady Climb

Source PDF: all report pdf/FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus  FEMO's Steady Climb.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus  FEMO's Steady Climb
Date:    5 hours ago
FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus FEMO's Steady Climb

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus
FEMO's Steady Climb
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 19 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Jonny Gios / Unsplash
I. FOMO vs FEMO
Forecasting the stock market is easy! Just multiply the P/E (price-to-
earnings ratio) by E (earnings per share) to get P (the stock price index).
The hard part, of course, is getting the P/E and E right. We call the P/E
component FOMO (fear of missing out) and the E component FEMO
(fabulous earnings momentum). This year, investors have been on a
rollercoaster ride as FOMO has fluctuated mostly to the downside, while

FEMO has continued climbing.

### Page 3

More recently, concerns about AI investment returns and rising US
Treasury yields have tightened credit conditions, raising fears about the
sustainability of the AI investment boom and weighing on P/Es. Since
early October, multiple compression has outpaced continued earnings
gains, dragging many stocks lower.
Semiconductors have been at the center of the recent rout, with the
semiconductor stock price index plunging 15.7% over eight consecutive
sessions. However, FOMO staged a comeback today, with the Nasdaq

rising 1.1% and semiconductors gaining 1.6%, snapping their losing
streak. The FOMO rollercoaster continues, but FEMO remains firmly on
an upward trajectory.
Here is more:
(1) S&P 500. So far this year, forward earnings surged 31.0% ytd through
October 19, more than offsetting a 14.2% contraction in the forward P/E.
Consequently, the index is up 12.3% ytd (chart).
(2) Information Technology. The divergence is even more striking in the
S&P 500 Information Technology sector. Forward earnings has soared

65.5% ytd, while the forward P/E has contracted 25.6%, leaving the
sector's stock price index up 23.0% ytd (chart). Since early October,
however, falling P/Es have outpaced rising earnings, pulling IT stock
prices lower.

### Page 4

(3) Semiconductors. FEMO is incredibly strong in the S&P 500
Semiconductors industry, which now accounts for a record 55.0% of the IT
sector's forward earnings and 40.9% of its market capitalization (chart).
That implies a significant forward P/E discount to the rest of the IT sector
and to the S&P 500. The discount reflects concerns that rapidly rising
borrowing costs for highly leveraged AI infrastructure providers could slow
the AI investment boom and weaken demand for chips. Semiconductor

earnings continue to soar, but that's because capacity constraints are
boosting semiconductor prices, which should fall sooner or later as either
demand slows or capacity expands, or both.
(4) Healthy constraints. We view investors' growing concerns about the
AI boom as good news. Financial, social, political, and physical
constraints should reduce overinvestment risks and keep the boom on a
sustainable path. Recently, financial constraints have been in focus, with

### Page 5

CoreWeave's bond yields surging above 15% last week as the Bond
Vigilantes increasingly scrutinized AI borrowers. The bond market is
playing a welcome role in allocating capital more efficiently. Meanwhile,
the big hyperscalers remain the boom's main drivers, supported by strong
cash flows and ample access to capital. The AI rollercoaster may be
hitting speed bumps, but that will help keep it on a steadier path.
II. Industrial Production
The strength of the US economy continues to underpin strong earnings

growth. Industrial production rose 0.7% m/m in September, its biggest
gain since April, and 2.2% y/y, the strongest since September 2022
(charts). Leading the way higher was utilities output. That reflects rising
electricity demand from data centers and manufacturing, as well as
weather-related demand. Manufacturing output edged up 0.3%.

### Page 6

Since ChatGPT was released in late 2022, technology-related industrial
production has risen sharply to record highs (chart).
Defense and space equipment production also continues to increase at a
solid pace, supported by rising military spending and the rapid growth of
the space industry (chart).

### Page 7

III. Federal Deficit
Fiscal policy also continues to boost economic growth, though at the cost
of large federal deficits and mounting federal debt. The federal budget
deficit totaled $1.99 trillion over the 12 months through September, while
marketable Treasury debt increased by $2.12 trillion (chart).
Federal outlays reached $7.40 trillion, including $3.77 trillion for health
care, Medicare, and Social Security (chart). Notably, net interest payments

reached a record $1.05 trillion, exceeding defense spending of $970
billion.

### Page 8

Tax receipts totaled $5.40 trillion, supported by rising individual income
and payroll taxes as well as tariff revenues (chart). Corporate income tax
receipts have been depressed this year by the 100% depreciation
allowance granted by last year's One Big Beautiful Bill Act (OBBB).
IV. Foreign Capital Flows
Given Corporate America's FEMO, it is not surprising that foreign capital
continues to pour into US financial markets. Over the 12 months through

August, net foreign purchases of US equities totaled a whopping $1.01
trillion, followed by corporate bonds and Treasury notes and bonds (chart).
Apparently, foreign investors have a greater appetite for US equities and
corporate bonds than for US Treasuries.

### Page 9

Net foreign equity inflows surged to a record high, far exceeding previous
peaks (chart). Private investors accounted for $844 billion, while foreign
official institutions contributed another $165 billion, a remarkable
development given their historically limited participation in US equity
markets.
Foreigners purchased more US corporate bonds than US Treasury notes
and bonds over the past 12 months (chart).

### Page 10

clause.
Yardeni QuickTakes © 2026 – Unsubscribe

## Document 352: Rates Spark_ Welcome to the 5% club

Source PDF: all report pdf/Rates Spark_ Welcome to the 5% club.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The yield on a 10Y French government bond easily pushed
through 5%, even though the rest of the market painted a
more positive picture. Despite wider spreads, markets are not
quick to turn more dovish on the ECB, but this could change
Michiel Tukker
With the recent unrest in France, the yield on the 10-year government bond is
at 5%
Markets not fazed by French bonds pushing
above 5%
The 10-year French government bond yield initially found some
resistance around 5% but easily broke that handle later in the day.

The sell-oﬀ continued as investors evaluated the impact of the
latest unrest. Meanwhile, other parts of the markets painted a more
positive picture. Equities were up and oil managed to nudge lower.
The divergence between France and the rest of the market suggests
contagion risks are still deemed minimal, in line with our own view.
Also, the front end of the euro swap curve was quite resilient to the
latest sell-oﬀ in French bonds, suggesting inﬂation risks are still the

SpaceXSI might reverse the negative sentiment on AI infrastructure investment

SpaceXSI 三⼤業務超預期，全線業務扭虧轉盈，有機會帶動之前弱

Executive summary
SpaceXSI 公布 3Q26 財報，三⼤業務  Starlink, ⽕箭， AI 營收，獲利⼤超預

該有機會成為  NeoCloud 新⿓頭。
Sales beat across all segments
SpaceXSI reports 3Q26 sales of US$14.575bn, up 87% qoq and
161% yoy, which was 13% above Bloomberg consensus estimates
of US$12.875bn. 3Q26 sales beat was across all segments with a

24% sales beat on Space, 8% beat on Starlink, and 20% beat on AI.
Operating proﬁts beat across all segments
SpaceXSI reports 3Q26 operating proﬁts of US$2.79bn at operating
margin of 19%, which was 131% higher than Bloomberg consensus
estimates of US$1.21bn at operating margin of 12% and turned
proﬁts from 2Q26’s -2% operating margin. We attributed the
improvement across all segments with 98% more losses cut on
Space vs. consensus, 27% beat on Starlink, and US$445mn higher

proﬁts on AI (-49% operating margin in 2Q26 to +6% in 3Q26),

US$3.54b.

Subscribed
Cutting capital density despite capex hike and negative free
cash ﬂow
Given SpaceXSI continues to boost its 3Q26 capex to US$19.593bn,
up 7% qoq and 185% yoy, coupled with short of cash ﬂow from

operations (US$9.63bn), the company reports a negative free cash
ﬂow of nearly US$10bn. However, due to a stronger than expected
sales improvement, capital density was cut to 134% in 3Q26 from
2Q26’s 235%.

### Page 3

Source: SpaceXSI, Andrew Lu on global semis/techs
Passing CoreWeave, catching Oracle on AI business
SpaceXSI reports US$7.56bn on AI business in 3Q26, up 195% qoq
and 830% yoy, growth accelerated from 2Q26’s 247% yoy. AI
business in 3Q26 accounted for 52% of SpaceXSI total sales, up
from 33% in 2Q26. As a result, we estimate SpaceXSI AI business to
exceed CoreWeave sales in 3Q26 and might exceed Oracle cloud
sales by 4Q27. Driven by a dramatic operating margin improvement

## Document 393: US SECTORS CALL_ Tech Wrecked, Health Care & Staples Rally

Source PDF: all report pdf/US SECTORS CALL_ Tech Wrecked, Health Care & Staples Rally.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US SECTORS CALL: Tech Wrecked, Health Care & Staples Rally
Date:    7 hours ago
US SECTORS CALL: Tech Wrecked, Health Care & Staples Rally

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US SECTORS CALL: Tech
Wrecked, Health Care & Staples
Rally
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 18 Oct 2026
View in browser

The S&P 500 fell 2.0% this week, and Information Technology (MW) took
the biggest hit. The sector fell 6.4%, the worst of the 11 sectors (chart).
The iShares Semiconductor ETF (SOXX) fell 11.8%. Credit worries spread
to AI infrastructure. CoreWeave fell 25% last week. Its worst day was
Wednesday, when it sank 15% as its bond yields soared. It was the IT
sector's second straight losing week. Industrials (OW) also fell for a
second week. On a ytd basis, Energy is up 52.7%, the best of the 11
sectors. IT is second at 21.6% (chart).

### Page 2

Consumer Staples (UW) and Energy (OW) led the 11 sectors, each up
5.0%. Health Care (OW) rose 3.4%. Every defensive sector gained, with
Utilities (MW) up 2.0% and Real Estate (UW) up 1.2%. Health Care and
Staples rallied for different reasons. Earnings drove Health Care's gains.
Staples' gains have been driven by its multiple.
Here's more on Information Technology, Health Care, and Consumer
Staples:
(1) Information Technology. The tech selloff last week was led by

Semiconductor Equipment (down 17.4%) and Communication Equipment
(down 13.3%) (chart). IT Consulting & Other Services rose 5.6%, and
Technology Hardware slipped only 1.5%.

### Page 3

Two weeks ago, Semiconductor Equipment was the best-performing IT
industry, up 97.3% ytd. It is now up 51.6%. Its forward P/E has fallen to
27.0 from 31.1. Its earnings outlook hasn't changed. Analysts still expect
growth of 35.0% this year and 48.5% next year (chart).
Semiconductor Equipment and Communication Equipment are the only IT
industries for which analysts expect faster growth next year (chart).
The selling hit the expensive industries. Semiconductor Equipment trades

at 27.0 times forward earnings and Communication Equipment at 25.2.
Semiconductors, the biggest contributor to IT's earnings, trades at a 14.7
multiple, below the S&P 500's 18.9 (chart). That is less than half its June
2024 peak of 35.5.

### Page 4

A low multiple can be a warning signal for a cyclical industry. Investors
tend to pay the least when earnings are peaking and the most when they
bottom. The industry's forward P/E reached 79.1 in February 2009, as
earnings collapsed. So far, analysts are still raising their estimates. They
expect earnings to grow 114.0% this year and 77.5% next year. As long as
estimates keep rising, we see this week's semiconductor selloff as a
buying opportunity. We retain our market weight rating on Information

Technology.
(2) Health Care. Health Care rose 3.4% this week, and every industry
gained. Health Care Distributors rose 16.1%. Even Health Care
Equipment, down 16.3% ytd, rose 4.4%.
Earnings are doing the heavy lifting. In August, forward earnings and the
forward P/E contributed about equally to the sector's ytd gain, at 6.6% and
6.3%, respectively. Forward earnings is up 9.2% ytd, while the forward P/E
is up only 2.8%. The price index is up 14.1% (chart).

### Page 5

The best is ahead. Analysts expect the sector’s earnings to grow 1.6% this
year and 22.5% next year. Pharmaceuticals' earnings are forecast to fall
5.2% this year and rise 28.6% next year (chart). Biotech is estimated to
grow earnings 46.4% next year after falling 6.2% this year.
The large caps are catching up. On September 10, the S&P 500 Health
Care index was up 6.7% ytd, against 20.6% for the S&P 600 and 17.2%
for the S&P 400. The S&P 500 sector is now up 14.1%, versus 17.8% and

17.3% (chart). The sector trades at 18.9 times forward earnings, equal to
the market multiple of 18.9. We retain our overweight rating on Health
Care.

### Page 6

(3) Consumer Staples. Two weeks ago, we argued that earnings didn't
justify Staples' valuation premium. Investors have since paid even more
for the sector. Its forward P/E has risen to 22.5 from 20.7 on October 1.
That is above both the S&P 500's 18.8 and IT's 19.1 (chart).
The multiple is doing the work again. Staples' forward P/E was down 0.3%
ytd at the end of September. Two weeks later, it is up 6.5% ytd. Forward
earnings is up 4.9% ytd and has barely moved since September (chart).

### Page 7

The priciest industry got pricier. Merchandise Retail's forward P/E rose to
33.6 from 31.0 two weeks ago, though its earnings growth is forecast to
slow to 7.1% in 2027 (chart). This is a defensive bid, not an earnings story.
We retain our underweight rating on Consumer Staples.
💡

log in to the Yardeni QuickTakes website and post them at the end
of the QuickTakes article. Paid members' contributions may be

featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

## Document 489: 三季度及9月宏观数据点评：增长动能边际回升，政策效果初步显现

Source PDF: all report pdf/三季度及9月宏观数据点评：增长动能边际回升，政策效果初步显现.pdf
Report source: 银泰证券
Date: 2026-10-21
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

请务必阅读正文之后的免责条款部分 研究专业创新 如意投资随心
宏观研究
宏观点评
增长动能边际回升，政策效果初步显现
——三季度及 9 月宏观数据点评
事件：2026 年 10 月 19 日，国家统计局发布三季度及 9 月份国内宏观经济
数据。数据显示，前三季度国内 GDP 同比增长 4.6%，其中三季度增长 4.5%；
9 月单月全国规模以上工业增加值同比实际增长 5.5%，社会消费品零售总额
同比增长 1.1%；1-9 月全国固定资产投资（不含农户）同比下降 7.2%，全国
房地产开发投资同比下降 20.6%。
核心观点：
 三季度国内 GDP增长 4.5%，增速较二季度的 4.3%回升 0.2个百分点，环
比增速由 0.9%加快至 1.2%，经济增长动能边际增强。9 月单月看，出口
高增背景下，工业生产增速进一步加快；中秋假期错位使得市场需求提前
释放，社零增速有所回升；固定资产投资维持低位，但得益于政策加速落
地，制造业投资边际改善；房地产开发各项指标延续调整，房价继续体现
出企稳信号。

 9月份以来，国内一系列稳增长政策相继部署落地，其效果初步显现。国
家发展改革委在 10 月 19 日新闻发布会上表示，国内经济稳定回升的基
础和势头偏弱，后期将进一步增强存量政策实施效能，协同推进“十五五”
规划、“六张网”实施方案重大工程项目开工建设，同时及时推出务实管
用的增量政策。若相关政策得到较好贯彻，四季度国内经济料仍有改善空
间，叠加上年同期低基数，全年 GDP增长目标有望顺利实现。
 风险提示：出口增速明显放缓；政策进度不及预期。
2026 年 10 月 20 日

1、三季度经济 GDP 增速好于预期
根据国家统计局发布的数据，2026 年前三季度
国内GDP累计增速为4.6%，其中三季度增长4.5%，
好于市场预期。前三季度国内 4.6%的 GDP 增速仍处
于年初设定的4.5%-5.0%目标区间内，鉴于近期稳增
长政策力度提升，四季度国内经济仍有改善空间，叠
加上年同期低基数，全年增长目标有望顺利实现。
单季度看，三季度 GDP 增速较二季度的 4.3%回
升0.2个百分点，环比增速由0.9%加快至1.2%，经
济增长动能边际企稳。分产业看，第一产业增加值同
比增长3.6%，对经济增长的贡献率为6.3%；第二产
业增加值同比增长 3.4%，对经济增长的贡献率为
27.2%；第三产业增加值同比增长5.2%，对经济增长
的贡献率为66.5%。
图表 1 国内 GDP 增速（%） 图表 2 三大产业对经济增长的贡献率（%）
数据来源：同花顺 iFinD、银泰证券研究所

三季度国内经济在增速企稳的同时，结构上不乏
亮点，主要体现在以下三个方面。一是服务业贡献加
大，前三季度第三产业增加值同比增长 5.2%，快于
整体经济0.6个百分点。二是新动能不断成长壮大，
向新向优稳步提速，前三季度装备制造业、高技术制
造业增加值分别增长10.2%和14.3%，分别快于全部
规上工业4.9和9.0个百分点。三是名义增速明显修
复，前三季度现价GDP同比增长约 5.5%，高于4.6%
的实际增速，其中三季度名义增速为6.0%，为近 18
个季度最高。

4、固投增速维持低位，制造业投资降幅收窄
1—9 月份，全国固定资产投资（不含农户）同
比下降 7.2%，降幅较上月持平，其中，民间固定资
产投资同比下降 10.4%，较上月降幅扩大 0.3个百分
点。从环比看，9 月份固定资产投资（不含农户）增
长 0.07%，自 3月份以来首次转正。
